@dataclass
class PositionState:
    symbol: str
    side: str  # "long" / "short"
    qty: float
    entry_price: float
    current_price: float
    unrealized_pnl_pct: float
    holding_days: float

@dataclass
class SignalSummary:
    symbol: str
    liquidation_risk_score: float
    wave_continuation_score: float
    snipe_opportunity_score: float
    details: dict

class PositionAnalyzer:
    def __init__(self, signal_sources):
        self.signal_sources = signal_sources  # ml, momentum, etc.

    def analyze_position(self, pos: PositionState, regime, market_context) -> SignalSummary:
        ml_signal = self.signal_sources["ml"].get_signal(pos.symbol)
        momentum_signal = self.signal_sources["momentum"].get_signal(pos.symbol)
        mr_signal = self.signal_sources["mean_reversion"].get_signal(pos.symbol)
        stat_arb_signal = self.signal_sources["stat_arb"].get_signal(pos.symbol)

        liquidation_risk = 0.0
        wave_continuation = 0.0
        snipe_opportunity = 0.0

        # Liquidation risk
        if pos.unrealized_pnl_pct < -market_context["max_dd_pct"]:
            liquidation_risk += 0.4
        if regime.trend == "down" and pos.side == "long":
            liquidation_risk += 0.3
        if ml_signal < 0 and momentum_signal < 0:
            liquidation_risk += 0.3

        # Wave continuation
        if regime.trend == "up" and pos.side == "long":
            wave_continuation += 0.4
        if ml_signal > 0 and momentum_signal > 0:
            wave_continuation += 0.4
        if market_context["volatility_in_range"]:
            wave_continuation += 0.2

        # Snipe opportunity (for symbols you don't hold or small size)
        if pos.qty == 0 or pos.qty < market_context["min_size_for_full"]:
            if ml_signal > market_context["ml_threshold"] and momentum_signal > market_context["mom_threshold"]:
                snipe_opportunity += 0.5
            if mr_signal < -market_context["mr_z_threshold"]:
                snipe_opportunity += 0.3
            if market_context["spread_bps"] < market_context["max_spread_bps"]:
                snipe_opportunity += 0.2

        return SignalSummary(
            symbol=pos.symbol,
            liquidation_risk_score=liquidation_risk,
            wave_continuation_score=wave_continuation,
            snipe_opportunity_score=snipe_opportunity,
            details={
                "ml_signal": ml_signal,
                "momentum_signal": momentum_signal,
                "mr_signal": mr_signal,
                "stat_arb_signal": stat_arb_signal,
                "regime": regime.__dict__,
                "position": pos.__dict__,
            }
        )
risk_profile_update(delta)

        wave_continuation = 0.0
        snipe_opportunity = 0.0

            liquidation_risk += 0.4
        if regime.trend == "down" and pos.side == "long":
            liquidation_risk += 0.3
        if ml_signal < 0 and momentum_signal < 0:
            liquidation_risk += 0.3

        # Wave continuation
        if regime.trend == "up" and pos.side == "long":
            wave_continuation += 0.4
        if ml_signal > 0 and momentum_signal > 0:
            wave_continuation += 0.4
        if market_context["volatility_in_range"]:
            wave_continuation += 0.2

            if ml_signal > market_context["ml_threshold"] and momentum_signal > market_context["mom_threshold"]:
                snipe_opportunity += 0.5

                snipe_opportunity += 0.3

                snipe_opportunity += 0.2

        return SignalSummary(
            symbol=pos.symbol,
            liquidation_risk_score=liquidation_risk,
            wave_continuation_score=wave_continuation,
            snipe_opportunity_score=snipe_opportunity,
            details={
                "ml_signal": ml_signal,
                "momentum_signal": momentum_signal,
                "mr_signal": mr_signal,
                "stat_arb_signal": stat_arb_signal,
                "regime": regime.__dict__,
                "position": pos.__dict__,
            }
        )
func (e *Executor) Boot(activate func(RunlevelName) error) error {
    for i, rl := range e.runlevels {
        e.current = i
        if err := activate(rl); err != nil {
            e.state = StateFailed
            return fmt.Errorf("runlevel %s failed: %w", rl, err)
        }
    }
    e.state = StateReady
    return nil
}

def corridor_failure(D_ij, delta_latency, baseline_latency, C_i, C_j,
                     alpha, beta, gamma, Z0, theta_class,
                     S_max, Z_critical):

    # Step 1: Stress tensor
    displacement_term = alpha * (np.linalg.norm(D_ij) ** 2)
    latency_term = beta * (delta_latency / baseline_latency)
    compute_term = gamma * (C_i * C_j)

    S_ij = displacement_term + latency_term + compute_term

    # Step 2: Dynamic impedance
    Z_ij = Z0 * np.exp(S_ij / theta_class)

    # Step 3: Failure condition
    failure = (S_ij > S_max) and (Z_ij > Z_critical)

    return {
        "stress": S_ij,
        "impedance": Z_ij,
        "failure": failure
    }

def corridor_failure(D_ij, delta_latency, baseline_latency, C_i, C_j,
                     alpha, beta, gamma, Z0, theta_class,
                     S_max, Z_critical):

    # Step 1: Stress tensor
    displacement_term = alpha * (np.linalg.norm(D_ij) ** 2)
    latency_term = beta * (delta_latency / baseline_latency)
    compute_term = gamma * (C_i * C_j)

    S_ij = displacement_term + latency_term + compute_term

    # Step 2: Dynamic impedance
    Z_ij = Z0 * np.exp(S_ij / theta_class)

    # Step 3: Failure condition
    failure = (S_ij > S_max) and (Z_ij > Z_critical)

    return {
        "stress": S_ij,
        "impedance": Z_ij,
        "failure": failure
    }

# d:/pqr.info/vault-adapter/adapter.py

import json
import os
from http.server import BaseHTTPRequestHandler, HTTPServer
import requests

SUBSTRATE_RPC_URL = os.getenv("SUBSTRATE_RPC_URL", "http://127.0.0.1:9933")

class VaultAdapter(BaseHTTPRequestHandler):

    def _send(self, code, payload):
        self.send_response(code)
        self.send_header("Content-Type", "application/json")
        self.end_headers()
        self.wfile.write(json.dumps(payload).encode())

    def do_GET(self):
        if not self.path.startswith("/v1/secret/data/"):
            return self._send(404, {"error": "Unsupported path"})

        secret_key = self.path.replace("/v1/secret/data/", "")
        vault_token = self.headers.get("X-Vault-Token", "")

        rpc_payload = {
            "jsonrpc": "2.0",
            "id": 1,
            "method": "vault_getSecret",
            "params": [secret_key]
        }

        try:
            rpc_response = requests.post(SUBSTRATE_RPC_URL, json=rpc_payload)
            rpc_json = rpc_response.json()
        except Exception as e:
            return self._send(500, {"error": f"RPC error: {str(e)}"})

        if "result" not in rpc_json:
            return self._send(404, {"error": "Secret not found"})

        vault_response = {
            "data": {
                "data": rpc_json["result"]
            }
        }

        self._send(200, vault_response)


def run():
    server = HTTPServer(("0.0.0.0", 8200), VaultAdapter)
    print("Vault Adapter running on port 8200...")
    server.serve_forever()


if __name__ == "__main__":
    run()

import numpy as np

def evaluate_high_tension_failure(
    D_ij, delta_latency, baseline_latency,
    C_i, C_j,
    alpha, beta, gamma,
    Z0, theta_HT,
    k_preload, S_max, Z_critical, P_limit
):
    # --- Step 1: Stress Tensor ---
    displacement_term = alpha * (np.linalg.norm(D_ij) ** 2)
    latency_term = beta * (delta_latency / baseline_latency)
    compute_term = gamma * (C_i * C_j)

    S_ij = displacement_term + latency_term + compute_term

    # --- Step 2: Dynamic Impedance ---
    Z_ij = Z0 * np.exp(S_ij / theta_HT)

    # --- Step 3: Preload Strain ---
    P_ij = k_preload * np.linalg.norm(D_ij)

    # --- Step 4: Failure Condition ---
    failure = (S_ij > S_max) and (Z_ij > Z_critical) and (P_ij > P_limit)

    return {
        "stress_tensor": S_ij,
        "impedance": Z_ij,
        "preload_strain": P_ij,
        "failure": failure
    }

import numpy as np

def evaluate_high_tension_failure(
    D_ij, delta_latency, baseline_latency,
    C_i, C_j,
    alpha, beta, gamma,
    Z0, theta_HT,
    k_preload, S_max, Z_critical, P_limit
):
    # --- Step 1: Stress Tensor ---
    displacement_term = alpha * (np.linalg.norm(D_ij) ** 2)
    latency_term = beta * (delta_latency / baseline_latency)
    compute_term = gamma * (C_i * C_j)

    S_ij = displacement_term + latency_term + compute_term

    # --- Step 2: Dynamic Impedance ---
    Z_ij = Z0 * np.exp(S_ij / theta_HT)

    # --- Step 3: Preload Strain ---
    P_ij = k_preload * np.linalg.norm(D_ij)

    # --- Step 4: Failure Condition ---
    failure = (S_ij > S_max) and (Z_ij > Z_critical) and (P_ij > P_limit)

    return {
        "stress_tensor": S_ij,
        "impedance": Z_ij,
        "preload_strain": P_ij,
        "failure": failure
    }

from collections import defaultdict

def build_capital_heatmap(snapshot: CapitalSnapshot):
    by_regime = defaultdict(float)
    by_strategy = defaultdict(float)
    by_liquidation_risk = defaultdict(float)
    by_wave_state = defaultdict(float)
    by_snipe_state = defaultdict(float)

    for exp in snapshot.exposures:
        by_regime[exp.regime] += exp.notional_value
        by_strategy[exp.strategy] += exp.notional_value

        # Bucket liquidation risk
        if exp.liquidation_risk_score >= 0.7:
            by_liquidation_risk["high"] += exp.notional_value
        elif exp.liquidation_risk_score >= 0.4:
            by_liquidation_risk["medium"] += exp.notional_value
        else:
            by_liquidation_risk["low"] += exp.notional_value

        # Bucket wave continuation
        if exp.wave_continuation_score >= 0.7:
            by_wave_state["strong_wave"] += exp.notional_value
        elif exp.wave_continuation_score >= 0.4:
            by_wave_state["moderate_wave"] += exp.notional_value
        else:
            by_wave_state["weak_or_none"] += exp.notional_value

        # Bucket snipe opportunity (for symbols you *could* enter more)
        if exp.snipe_opportunity_score >= 0.7:
            by_snipe_state["high_opportunity"] += exp.notional_value
        elif exp.snipe_opportunity_score >= 0.4:
            by_snipe_state["medium_opportunity"] += exp.notional_value
        else:
            by_snipe_state["low_opportunity"] += exp.notional_value

    return {
        "by_regime": dict(by_regime),
        "by_strategy": dict(by_strategy),
        "by_liquidation_risk": dict(by_liquidation_risk),
        "by_wave_state": dict(by_wave_state),
        "by_snipe_state": dict(by_snipe_state),
        "cash_balance": snapshot.cash_balance,
        "total_equity": snapshot.total_equity,
    }

func RunInterpreter(ctx context.Context, bio *Biographer, rt *RTClient) {
    ticker := time.NewTicker(5 * time.Minute)
    defer ticker.Stop()

    for {
        select {
        case <-ctx.Done():
            return
        case <-ticker.C:
            patterns := analyzeLineage()
            for _, p := range patterns {
                emitInsight(ctx, bio, rt, p)
            }
        }
    }
}

pub struct VaultRpcHandler<C, P> {
    client: Arc<C>,
    pool: Arc<P>,
}

impl<C, P> VaultRpc for VaultRpcHandler<C, P>
where
    C: ProvideRuntimeApi<Block> + HeaderBackend<Block>,
    C::Api: VaultApi<Block>,
{
    fn vault_getSecret(&self, key: String) -> Result<HashMap<String, String>> {
        let api = self.client.runtime_api();
        let result = api.vault_get_secret(key.into_bytes());

        if let Some(map) = result {
            let mut out = HashMap::new();
            for (k, v) in map {
                out.insert(
                    String::from_utf8_lossy(&k).to_string(),
                    String::from_utf8_lossy(&v).to_string(),
                );
            }
            Ok(out)
        } else {
            Ok(HashMap::new())
        }
    }
}

#![cfg_attr(not(feature = "std"), no_std)]

pub use pallet::*;

#[frame_support::pallet]
pub mod pallet {
    use frame_support::{
        pallet_prelude::*,
    };
    use frame_system::pallet_prelude::*;
    use sp_std::collections::btree_map::BTreeMap;

    #[pallet::config]
    pub trait Config: frame_system::Config {
        type RuntimeEvent: From<Event<Self>> + IsType<<Self as frame_system::Config>::RuntimeEvent>;
        type MaxKeyLength: Get<u32>;
        type MaxValueLength: Get<u32>;
    }

    #[pallet::storage]
    #[pallet::getter(fn vault_storage)]
    pub type VaultStorage<T: Config> = StorageMap<
        _,
        Blake2_128Concat,
        BoundedVec<u8, T::MaxKeyLength>,
        BTreeMap<BoundedVec<u8, T::MaxKeyLength>, BoundedVec<u8, T::MaxValueLength>>,
        OptionQuery,
    >;

    #[pallet::event]
    pub enum Event<T: Config> {
        SecretSet(BoundedVec<u8, T::MaxKeyLength>),
    }

    #[pallet::error]
    pub enum Error<T> {
        KeyTooLong,
        ValueTooLong,
    }

    #[pallet::call]
    impl<T: Config> Pallet<T> {
        #[pallet::call_index(0)]
        #[pallet::weight(10_000)]
        pub fn set_secret(
            origin: OriginFor<T>,
            key: BoundedVec<u8, T::MaxKeyLength>,
            entries: BTreeMap<BoundedVec<u8, T::MaxKeyLength>, BoundedVec<u8, T::MaxValueLength>>,
        ) -> DispatchResult {
            let _who = ensure_signed(origin)?;
            VaultStorage::<T>::insert(&key, entries);
            Self::deposit_event(Event::SecretSet(key));
            Ok(())
        }
    }

    // Helper for runtime API: convert raw Vec<u8> key to stored map
    impl<T: Config> Pallet<T> {
        pub fn get_secret_raw(key: Vec<u8>) -> Option<BTreeMap<Vec<u8>, Vec<u8>>> {
            let bounded_key =
                BoundedVec::<u8, T::MaxKeyLength>::try_from(key.clone()).ok()?;
            let stored = VaultStorage::<T>::get(bounded_key)?;
            let mut out = BTreeMap::new();
            for (k, v) in stored.into_iter() {
                out.insert(k.into_inner(), v.into_inner());
            }
            Some(out)
        }
    }
}

market_data
type Executor struct {
    state     State
    runlevels []RunlevelName
    current   int
    history   []RunlevelName
}

func (e *Executor) Boot(activate func(RunlevelName) error) error {
    e.state = StateBooting

    for i, rl := range e.runlevels {
        e.current = i

        if err := activate(rl); err != nil {
            e.state = StateFailed
            return fmt.Errorf("runlevel %s failed: %w", rl, err)
        }

        e.history = append(e.history, rl)
    }

    e.state = StateReady
    return nil
}

impl<T: Config> Pallet<T> {
    fn run_offchain_worker(_block_number: BlockNumberFor<T>) -> Result<(), &'static str> {
        // 1. Fetch all active Vault-lane leases from storage
        let leases: Vec<Lease<T>> = Leases::<T>::iter()
            .filter(|(_, lease)| lease.role == ROLE_VAULT_ADAPTER)
            .map(|(_, lease)| lease)
            .collect();

        // 2. Compute 8NN for each addr5d
        for lease in leases.iter() {
            let addr = lease.addr5d.clone();
            let neighbors = Self::compute_8nn(&addr, &leases);

            // 3. Build route hints for each neighbor
            let hints = neighbors
                .into_iter()
                .map(|n| RouteHint::<T> {
                    from_addr5d: addr.clone(),
                    to_addr5d: n.addr5d.clone(),
                    hops: vec![n.addr5d.clone()], // start simple: direct hop
                })
                .collect::<Vec<_>>();

            // 4. Submit unsigned tx to update RouteHints on-chain
            Self::submit_route_hints_update(addr.clone(), hints)?;
        }

        Ok(())
    }

    fn compute_8nn(
        addr: &BoundedVec<u8, T::MaxAddr5dLength>,
        leases: &[Lease<T>],
    ) -> Vec<Lease<T>> {
        let mut scored = leases
            .iter()
            .filter(|l| &l.addr5d != addr)
            .map(|l| {
                let d = Self::distance(addr, &l.addr5d);
                (d, l.clone())
            })
            .collect::<Vec<_>>();

        scored.sort_by_key(|(d, _)| *d);
        scored.into_iter().take(8).map(|(_, l)| l).collect()
    }

    fn distance(
        a: &BoundedVec<u8, T::MaxAddr5dLength>,
        b: &BoundedVec<u8, T::MaxAddr5dLength>,
    ) -> u32 {
        // simple Hamming distance over bytes
        a.iter()
            .zip(b.iter())
            .map(|(x, y)| if x == y { 0 } else { 1 })
            .sum()
    }

    fn submit_route_hints_update(
        addr: BoundedVec<u8, T::MaxAddr5dLength>,
        hints: Vec<RouteHint<T>>,
    ) -> Result<(), &'static str> {
        use frame_system::offchain::SubmitTransaction;
        let call = Call::<T>::push_route_hint_batch { addr5d: addr, hints };
        SubmitTransaction::<T, Call<T>>::submit_unsigned_transaction(call.into())
            .map_err(|_| "submit failed")
    }
}

impl<T: Config> Pallet<T> {
    fn run_offchain_worker(_block_number: BlockNumberFor<T>) -> Result<(), &'static str> {
        // 1. Fetch all active Vault-lane leases from storage
        let leases: Vec<Lease<T>> = Leases::<T>::iter()
            .filter(|(_, lease)| lease.role == ROLE_VAULT_ADAPTER)
            .map(|(_, lease)| lease)
            .collect();

        // 2. Compute 8NN for each addr5d
        for lease in leases.iter() {
            let addr = lease.addr5d.clone();
            let neighbors = Self::compute_8nn(&addr, &leases);

            // 3. Build route hints for each neighbor
            let hints = neighbors
                .into_iter()
                .map(|n| RouteHint::<T> {
                    from_addr5d: addr.clone(),
                    to_addr5d: n.addr5d.clone(),
                    hops: vec![n.addr5d.clone()], // start simple: direct hop
                })
                .collect::<Vec<_>>();

            // 4. Submit unsigned tx to update RouteHints on-chain
            Self::submit_route_hints_update(addr.clone(), hints)?;
        }

        Ok(())
    }

    fn compute_8nn(
        addr: &BoundedVec<u8, T::MaxAddr5dLength>,
        leases: &[Lease<T>],
    ) -> Vec<Lease<T>> {
        let mut scored = leases
            .iter()
            .filter(|l| &l.addr5d != addr)
            .map(|l| {
                let d = Self::distance(addr, &l.addr5d);
                (d, l.clone())
            })
            .collect::<Vec<_>>();

        scored.sort_by_key(|(d, _)| *d);
        scored.into_iter().take(8).map(|(_, l)| l).collect()
    }

    fn distance(
        a: &BoundedVec<u8, T::MaxAddr5dLength>,
        b: &BoundedVec<u8, T::MaxAddr5dLength>,
    ) -> u32 {
        // simple Hamming distance over bytes
        a.iter()
            .zip(b.iter())
            .map(|(x, y)| if x == y { 0 } else { 1 })
            .sum()
    }

    fn submit_route_hints_update(
        addr: BoundedVec<u8, T::MaxAddr5dLength>,
        hints: Vec<RouteHint<T>>,
    ) -> Result<(), &'static str> {
        use frame_system::offchain::SubmitTransaction;
        let call = Call::<T>::push_route_hint_batch { addr5d: addr, hints };
        SubmitTransaction::<T, Call<T>>::submit_unsigned_transaction(call.into())
            .map_err(|_| "submit failed")
    }
}

impl<T: Config> Pallet<T> {
    fn run_offchain_worker(_block_number: BlockNumberFor<T>) -> Result<(), &'static str> {
        // 1. Fetch all active Vault-lane leases from storage
        let leases: Vec<Lease<T>> = Leases::<T>::iter()
            .filter(|(_, lease)| lease.role == ROLE_VAULT_ADAPTER)
            .map(|(_, lease)| lease)
            .collect();

        // 2. Compute 8NN for each addr5d
        for lease in leases.iter() {
            let addr = lease.addr5d.clone();
            let neighbors = Self::compute_8nn(&addr, &leases);

            // 3. Build route hints for each neighbor
            let hints = neighbors
                .into_iter()
                .map(|n| RouteHint::<T> {
                    from_addr5d: addr.clone(),
                    to_addr5d: n.addr5d.clone(),
                    hops: vec![n.addr5d.clone()], // start simple: direct hop
                })
                .collect::<Vec<_>>();

            // 4. Submit unsigned tx to update RouteHints on-chain
            Self::submit_route_hints_update(addr.clone(), hints)?;
        }

        Ok(())
    }

    fn compute_8nn(
        addr: &BoundedVec<u8, T::MaxAddr5dLength>,
        leases: &[Lease<T>],
    ) -> Vec<Lease<T>> {
        let mut scored = leases
            .iter()
            .filter(|l| &l.addr5d != addr)
            .map(|l| {
                let d = Self::distance(addr, &l.addr5d);
                (d, l.clone())
            })
            .collect::<Vec<_>>();

        scored.sort_by_key(|(d, _)| *d);
        scored.into_iter().take(8).map(|(_, l)| l).collect()
    }

    fn distance(
        a: &BoundedVec<u8, T::MaxAddr5dLength>,
        b: &BoundedVec<u8, T::MaxAddr5dLength>,
    ) -> u32 {
        // simple Hamming distance over bytes
        a.iter()
            .zip(b.iter())
            .map(|(x, y)| if x == y { 0 } else { 1 })
            .sum()
    }

    fn submit_route_hints_update(
        addr: BoundedVec<u8, T::MaxAddr5dLength>,
        hints: Vec<RouteHint<T>>,
    ) -> Result<(), &'static str> {
        use frame_system::offchain::SubmitTransaction;
        let call = Call::<T>::push_route_hint_batch { addr5d: addr, hints };
        SubmitTransaction::<T, Call<T>>::submit_unsigned_transaction(call.into())
            .map_err(|_| "submit failed")
    }
}

#[pallet::call]
impl<T: Config> Pallet<T> {
    #[pallet::call_index(3)]
    pub fn push_route_hint_batch(
        origin: OriginFor<T>,
        addr5d: BoundedVec<u8, T::MaxAddr5dLength>,
        hints: Vec<RouteHint<T>>,
    ) -> DispatchResult {
        ensure_none(origin)?; // unsigned, off-chain only
        RouteHints::<T>::insert(addr5d, hints);
        Ok(())
    }
}

engine/trade_engine.py
fn distance(a: &Addr5D, b: &Addr5D, mass_b: u32) -> u32 {
    let hamming = a.bytes().iter()
        .zip(b.bytes().iter())
        .map(|(x, y)| if x == y { 0 } else { 1 })
        .sum::<u32>();

    // gravity well: lower distance for high-mass nodes
    hamming.saturating_sub(mass_b)
}

package brokerage

import (
    "encoding/json"
    "os"
)

type Position struct {
    Ticker      string  `json:"ticker"`
    Quantity    float64 `json:"quantity"`
    AvgCost     float64 `json:"avg_cost"`
    MarketValue float64 `json:"market_value,omitempty"`
}

type PositionsFile struct {
    Positions []Position `json:"positions"`
}

func LoadPositions(path string) ([]Position, error) {
    b, err := os.ReadFile(path)
    if err != nil {
        return nil, err
    }
    var pf PositionsFile
    if err := json.Unmarshal(b, &pf); err != nil {
        return nil, err
    }
    return pf.Positions, nil
}

package brokerage

import (
    "encoding/json"
    "os"
)

type Position struct {
    Ticker      string  `json:"ticker"`
    Quantity    float64 `json:"quantity"`
    AvgCost     float64 `json:"avg_cost"`
    MarketValue float64 `json:"market_value,omitempty"`
}

type PositionsFile struct {
    Positions []Position `json:"positions"`
}

func LoadPositions(path string) ([]Position, error) {
    b, err := os.ReadFile(path)
    if err != nil {
        return nil, err
    }
    var pf PositionsFile
    if err := json.Unmarshal(b, &pf); err != nil {
        return nil, err
    }
    return pf.Positions, nil
}

onnxruntime
fn compute_8nn(addr: &Addr5D, leases: &[Lease], hosts: &[HostProfile]) -> Vec<Lease> {
    let mut scored = leases.iter().map(|l| {
        let host = hosts.iter().find(|h| h.host_id == l.host_id).unwrap();
        let d = distance(addr, &l.addr5d, host.mass);
        (d, l.clone())
    }).collect::<Vec<_>>();

    scored.sort_by_key(|(d, _)| *d);
    scored.into_iter().take(8).map(|(_, l)| l).collect()
}

fn build_route_hints(addr: &Addr5D, nn8: &[Lease]) -> Vec<RouteHint> {
    nn8.iter().map(|neighbor| {
        RouteHint {
            from_addr5d: addr.clone(),
            to_addr5d: neighbor.addr5d.clone(),
            hops: vec![neighbor.addr5d.clone()], // direct hop for now
        }
    }).collect()
}

fn run_offchain_worker() {
    let leases = all_vault_lane_leases();
    let hosts = all_hosts();

    for lease in leases {
        let nn8 = compute_8nn(&lease.addr5d, &leases, &hosts);
        let hints = build_route_hints(&lease.addr5d, &nn8);
        submit_route_hint_batch(lease.addr5d.clone(), hints);
    }
}

package data

import (
    "time"
)

type OHLCV struct {
    Timestamp time.Time `json:"timestamp"`
    Open      float64   `json:"open"`
    High      float64   `json:"high"`
    Low       float64   `json:"low"`
    Close     float64   `json:"close"`
    Volume    float64   `json:"volume"`
}

func GetRecentOHLCV(ticker string, days int) ([]OHLCV, error) {
    end := time.Now()
    start := end.AddDate(0, 0, -days)
    return GetOHLCV("data/stooq.db", ticker, start, end)
}

package data

import (
    "time"
)

type OHLCV struct {
    Timestamp time.Time `json:"timestamp"`
    Open      float64   `json:"open"`
    High      float64   `json:"high"`
    Low       float64   `json:"low"`
    Close     float64   `json:"close"`
    Volume    float64   `json:"volume"`
}

func GetRecentOHLCV(ticker string, days int) ([]OHLCV, error) {
    end := time.Now()
    start := end.AddDate(0, 0, -days)
    return GetOHLCV("data/stooq.db", ticker, start, end)
}

package data

import (
    "time"
)

type OHLCV struct {
    Timestamp time.Time `json:"timestamp"`
    Open      float64   `json:"open"`
    High      float64   `json:"high"`
    Low       float64   `json:"low"`
    Close     float64   `json:"close"`
    Volume    float64   `json:"volume"`
}

func GetRecentOHLCV(ticker string, days int) ([]OHLCV, error) {
    end := time.Now()
    start := end.AddDate(0, 0, -days)
    return GetOHLCV("data/stooq.db", ticker, start, end)
}

package main

import (
    "encoding/json"
    "flag"
    "fmt"
    "os"

    "jetweb/brokerage"
    "jetweb/data"
)

type Output struct {
    Positions  []brokerage.Position        `json:"positions"`
    MarketData map[string][]data.OHLCV     `json:"market_data"`
}

func main() {
    brokerURL := flag.String("broker", "https://app.alpaca.markets/portfolio", "Brokerage portal URL")
    days := flag.Int("days", 30, "Number of days of OHLCV to fetch")
    flag.Parse()

    fmt.Println("Broker URL:", *brokerURL)
    fmt.Println("Days:", *days)
    fmt.Println(">> Please ensure Chrome is running with remote debugging and the Cobrowse sub-agent has scraped positions.json")

    positions, err := brokerage.LoadPositions("output/positions.json")
    if err != nil {
        panic(fmt.Errorf("failed to load positions: %w", err))
    }

    out := Output{
        Positions:  positions,
        MarketData: make(map[string][]data.OHLCV),
    }

    for _, p := range positions {
        ohlcv, err := data.GetRecentOHLCV(p.Ticker, *days)
        if err != nil {
            fmt.Printf("warning: failed to fetch OHLCV for %s: %v\n", p.Ticker, err)
            continue
        }
        out.MarketData[p.Ticker] = ohlcv
    }

    b, err := json.MarshalIndent(out, "", "  ")
    if err != nil {
        panic(fmt.Errorf("failed to marshal output: %w", err))
    }

    if err := os.WriteFile("output/positions_with_market.json", b, 0o644); err != nil {
        panic(fmt.Errorf("failed to write output: %w", err))
    }

    fmt.Println("Wrote output/positions_with_market.json")
}

from dataclasses import dataclass
import numpy as np

@dataclass
class InferenceRequest:
    model_name: str
    features: np.ndarray  # shape: (feature_dim,)
    context: dict         # symbol, timeframe, etc.

@dataclass
class InferenceResult:
    signal: float         # e.g., expected return or score
    confidence: float
    metadata: dict        # debug info, regime, etc.
{
  "src": addr5d,
  "dst": addr5d,
  "epoch": <u64>,
  "nonce": <u64>,
  "payload": <encrypted bytes>,
  "auth": <signature>
}
func RunInterpreter(ctx context.Context, bio *Biographer, rt *RTClient) {
    ticker := time.NewTicker(5 * time.Minute)
    defer ticker.Stop()

    for {
        select {
        case <-ctx.Done():
            return
        case <-ticker.C:
            insights := analyzeLineage()
            for _, ins := range insights {
                emitInsight(ctx, bio, rt, ins)
            }
        }
    }
}
func RunInterpreter(ctx context.Context, bio *Biographer, rt *RTClient) {
    ticker := time.NewTicker(5 * time.Minute)
    defer ticker.Stop()

    for {
        select {
        case <-ctx.Done():
            return
        case <-ticker.C:
            insights := analyzeLineage()
            for _, ins := range insights {
                emitInsight(ctx, bio, rt, ins)
            }
        }
    }
}

# tests/test_vault_adapter.py

import requests

def test_vault_adapter_imagefx():
    r = requests.get(
        "http://localhost:8200/v1/secret/data/imagefx",
        headers={"X-Vault-Token": "test"},
        timeout=3,
    )
    assert r.status_code == 200
    j = r.json()
    assert "data" in j
    assert "data" in j["data"]
    assert "api_key" in j["data"]["data"]

# inference/server.py
import onnxruntime as ort
from typing import Dict
from .types import InferenceRequest, InferenceResult

class InferenceServer:
    def __init__(self, model_paths: Dict[str, str]):
        self.sessions = {
            name: ort.InferenceSession(path)
            for name, path in model_paths.items()
        }

    def predict(self, req: InferenceRequest) -> InferenceResult:
        session = self.sessions[req.model_name]
        # Map features to ONNX input
        inputs = {"input": req.features.astype("float32")}
        outputs = session.run(None, inputs)
        # Assume first output is signal
        signal = float(outputs[0][0])
        confidence = float(outputs[1][0]) if len(outputs) > 1 else 1.0
        return InferenceResult(
            signal=signal,
            confidence=confidence,
            metadata={"raw_outputs": outputs}
        )

def bootstrap_inference_server() -> InferenceServer:
    model_paths = {
        "lstm_price_forecaster": "models/compiled/lstm_price_forecaster.onnx",
    }
    return InferenceServer(model_paths)

# inference/server.py
import onnxruntime as ort
from typing import Dict
from .types import InferenceRequest, InferenceResult

class InferenceServer:
    def __init__(self, model_paths: Dict[str, str]):
        self.sessions = {
            name: ort.InferenceSession(path)
            for name, path in model_paths.items()
        }

    def predict(self, req: InferenceRequest) -> InferenceResult:
        session = self.sessions[req.model_name]
        # Map features to ONNX input
        inputs = {"input": req.features.astype("float32")}
        outputs = session.run(None, inputs)
        # Assume first output is signal
        signal = float(outputs[0][0])
        confidence = float(outputs[1][0]) if len(outputs) > 1 else 1.0
        return InferenceResult(
            signal=signal,
            confidence=confidence,
            metadata={"raw_outputs": outputs}
        )

def bootstrap_inference_server() -> InferenceServer:
    model_paths = {
        "lstm_price_forecaster": "models/compiled/lstm_price_forecaster.onnx",
    }
    return InferenceServer(model_paths)

Gemma-Cobrowse Positions & Market Data Workflow

Prereqs:
- Chrome installed
- Port 9222 free
- Stooq SQLite at data/stooq.db
- Node Copilot bridge at C:\Users\theal\copilot-bridge\mcp_receiver.js

Steps:
1. Start Chrome with remote debugging:
   chrome.exe --remote-debugging-port=9222

2. Start the Copilot bridge:
   node C:\Users\theal\copilot-bridge\mcp_receiver.js

3. Run the positions fetch CLI:
   go run cmd/positions_fetch/main.go --broker https://app.alpaca.markets/portfolio --days 30

4. In the opened Chrome window, log into your brokerage (2FA, captchas, etc.).

5. Wait for the sub-agent to scrape positions and write output/positions.json.

6. The CLI will then read positions.json, fetch OHLCV from data/stooq.db, and write:
   output/positions_with_market.json

7. Feed positions_with_market.json to your predictive-analysis agents.

use substrate_test_runtime::Runtime;
use sp_std::collections::btree_map::BTreeMap;

#[test]
fn vault_api_returns_secret() {
    // Arrange: insert into VaultStorage
    let key = b"imagefx".to_vec();
    let mut map = BTreeMap::new();
    map.insert(b"api_key".to_vec(), b"xyz".to_vec());

    pallet_vault_storage::VaultStorage::<Runtime>::insert(
        key.clone().try_into().unwrap(),
        map.clone()
    );

    // Act
    let result = pallet_vault_storage::Pallet::<Runtime>::get_secret_raw(key).unwrap();

    // Assert
    assert_eq!(result.get(&b"api_key".to_vec()).unwrap(), &b"xyz".to_vec());
}

# inference/models/lstm_predictor.py
import torch
import torch.nn as nn

class LSTMPredictor(nn.Module):
    def __init__(self, input_dim: int, hidden_dim: int, num_layers: int, output_dim: int):
        super().__init__()
        self.lstm = nn.LSTM(input_dim, hidden_dim, num_layers, batch_first=True)
        self.fc = nn.Linear(hidden_dim, output_dim)

    def forward(self, x):
        # x: (batch, seq_len, input_dim)
        out, _ = self.lstm(x)
        last = out[:, -1, :]
        return self.fc(last)

def export_to_onnx(model: LSTMPredictor, path: str, seq_len: int, input_dim: int):
    dummy_input = torch.randn(1, seq_len, input_dim)
    torch.onnx.export(
        model,
        dummy_input,
        path,
        input_names=["input"],
        output_names=["signal"],
        dynamic_axes={"input": {0: "batch_size", 1: "seq_len"}},
        opset_version=17,
    )

import torch.nn as nn

class LSTMPredictor(nn.Module):
    def __init__(self, input_dim: int, hidden_dim: int, num_layers: int, output_dim: int):
        super().__init__()
        self.lstm = nn.LSTM(input_dim, hidden_dim, num_layers, batch_first=True)
        self.fc = nn.Linear(hidden_dim, output_dim)

    def forward(self, x):
        # x: (batch, seq_len, input_dim)
        out, _ = self.lstm(x)
        last = out[:, -1, :]
        return self.fc(last)

def export_to_onnx(model: LSTMPredictor, path: str, seq_len: int, input_dim: int):
    dummy_input = torch.randn(1, seq_len, input_dim)
    torch.onnx.export(
        model,
        dummy_input,
        path,
        input_names=["input"],
        output_names=["signal"],
        dynamic_axes={"input": {0: "batch_size", 1: "seq_len"}},
        opset_version=17,
    )

func TestLoadPositions(t *testing.T) {
    positions, err := LoadPositions("testdata/positions_sample.json")
    if err != nil {
        t.Fatalf("LoadPositions failed: %v", err)
    }
    if len(positions) == 0 {
        t.Fatalf("expected positions, got none")
    }
}

# engine/trade_engine.py
from concurrent.futures import ProcessPoolExecutor
from typing import List, Callable
from .types import Bar
from .ledger import LedgerProcess
from .algos import mean_reversion, momentum, stat_arb, ml_predictor

class TradeEngine:
    def __init__(self, ledger: LedgerProcess, inference_server_factory: Callable):
        self.ledger = ledger
        self.inference_server_factory = inference_server_factory
        self.executor = ProcessPoolExecutor(max_workers=4)  # tuned for Max

    def start(self, market_data_stream):
        # Spawn ledger
        self.ledger.start()

        # Spawn algorithm workers
        algos = [
            mean_reversion.MeanReversionAlgo(),
            momentum.MomentumAlgo(),
            stat_arb.StatArbAlgo(),
            ml_predictor.MLPredictorAlgo(self.inference_server_factory),
        ]

        for algo in algos:
            self.executor.submit(self.run_algo_loop, algo, market_data_stream)

    def run_algo_loop(self, algo, market_data_stream):
        for bar in market_data_stream:
            orders = algo.on_bar(bar)
            for order in orders:
                self.ledger.submit_order(order)

# engine/trade_engine.py
from concurrent.futures import ProcessPoolExecutor
from typing import List, Callable
from .types import Bar
from .ledger import LedgerProcess
from .algos import mean_reversion, momentum, stat_arb, ml_predictor

class TradeEngine:
    def __init__(self, ledger: LedgerProcess, inference_server_factory: Callable):
        self.ledger = ledger
        self.inference_server_factory = inference_server_factory
        self.executor = ProcessPoolExecutor(max_workers=4)  # tuned for Max

    def start(self, market_data_stream):
        # Spawn ledger
        self.ledger.start()

        # Spawn algorithm workers
        algos = [
            mean_reversion.MeanReversionAlgo(),
            momentum.MomentumAlgo(),
            stat_arb.StatArbAlgo(),
            ml_predictor.MLPredictorAlgo(self.inference_server_factory),
        ]

        for algo in algos:
            self.executor.submit(self.run_algo_loop, algo, market_data_stream)

    def run_algo_loop(self, algo, market_data_stream):
        for bar in market_data_stream:
            orders = algo.on_bar(bar)
            for order in orders:
                self.ledger.submit_order(order)

# vault-adapter/discovery.py

import os
import random
import dns.resolver

DEFAULT_URL = os.getenv("SUBSTRATE_RPC_URL", "http://127.0.0.1:9933")

def discover_node():
    try:
        answers = dns.resolver.resolve("_substrate._tcp.mesh.pqr.info", "SRV")
        candidates = []
        for rdata in answers:
            host = str(rdata.target).rstrip(".")
            port = rdata.port
            candidates.append(f"http://{host}:{port}")
        if not candidates:
            return DEFAULT_URL
        return random.choice(candidates)
    except Exception:
        return DEFAULT_URL

# algos/ml_predictor.py
from typing import List
from ..types import Bar, OrderEvent, Side, OrderType
from ..inference.types import InferenceRequest
from datetime import datetime

class MLPredictorAlgo:
    def __init__(self, inference_server_factory):
        self.inference_server = inference_server_factory()
        self.name = "ml_predictor"

    def build_features(self, bar: Bar):
        # Simple feature vector; extend later
        return np.array([
            bar.open, bar.high, bar.low, bar.close, bar.volume
        ], dtype="float32")

    def on_bar(self, bar: Bar) -> List[OrderEvent]:
        features = self.build_features(bar)
        req = InferenceRequest(
            model_name="lstm_price_forecaster",
            features=features,
            context={"symbol": bar.symbol}
        )
        result = self.inference_server.predict(req)

        orders: List[OrderEvent] = []
        if result.signal > 0.0:
            side = Side.BUY
        elif result.signal < 0.0:
            side = Side.SELL
        else:
            return orders

        orders.append(OrderEvent(
            id=f"{self.name}-{datetime.utcnow().isoformat()}",
            source_algo=self.name,
            broker="schwab_sim",
            symbol=bar.symbol,
            side=side,
            qty=10,
            order_type=OrderType.MARKET,
            limit_price=None,
            timestamp=datetime.utcnow(),
            metadata={"signal": result.signal, "confidence": result.confidence}
        ))
        return orders

# algos/ml_predictor.py
from typing import List
from ..types import Bar, OrderEvent, Side, OrderType
from ..inference.types import InferenceRequest
from datetime import datetime

class MLPredictorAlgo:
    def __init__(self, inference_server_factory):
        self.inference_server = inference_server_factory()
        self.name = "ml_predictor"

    def build_features(self, bar: Bar):
        # Simple feature vector; extend later
        return np.array([
            bar.open, bar.high, bar.low, bar.close, bar.volume
        ], dtype="float32")

    def on_bar(self, bar: Bar) -> List[OrderEvent]:
        features = self.build_features(bar)
        req = InferenceRequest(
            model_name="lstm_price_forecaster",
            features=features,
            context={"symbol": bar.symbol}
        )
        result = self.inference_server.predict(req)

        orders: List[OrderEvent] = []
        if result.signal > 0.0:
            side = Side.BUY
        elif result.signal < 0.0:
            side = Side.SELL
        else:
            return orders

        orders.append(OrderEvent(
            id=f"{self.name}-{datetime.utcnow().isoformat()}",
            source_algo=self.name,
            broker="schwab_sim",
            symbol=bar.symbol,
            side=side,
            qty=10,
            order_type=OrderType.MARKET,
            limit_price=None,
            timestamp=datetime.utcnow(),
            metadata={"signal": result.signal, "confidence": result.confidence}
        ))
        return orders

# ledger/process.py
from multiprocessing import Process, Queue
from .types import OrderEvent, LedgerEvent, LedgerEventType
from datetime import datetime

class LedgerProcess:
    def __init__(self):
        self.order_queue: Queue = Queue()
        self.event_log: list[LedgerEvent] = []

    def start(self):
        self.proc = Process(target=self._run)
        self.proc.start()

    def submit_order(self, order: OrderEvent):
        self.order_queue.put(order)

    def _run(self):
        while True:
            order: OrderEvent = self.order_queue.get()
            # Simulate latency, fills, etc.
            submitted_event = LedgerEvent(
                id=f"ledger-{order.id}-submitted",
                event_type=LedgerEventType.ORDER_SUBMITTED,
                timestamp=datetime.utcnow(),
                payload={"order": order.__dict__},
                source=order.broker,
            )
            self.event_log.append(submitted_event)
            # TODO: simulate fill and position updates

from .types import OrderEvent, LedgerEvent, LedgerEventType
from datetime import datetime

class LedgerProcess:

        self.proc = Process(target=self._run)
        self.proc.start()

    def submit_order(self, order: OrderEvent):
        self.order_queue.put(order)

    def _run(self):
        while True:
            order: OrderEvent = self.order_queue.get()
            # Simulate latency, fills, etc.
            submitted_event = LedgerEvent(
                id=f"ledger-{order.id}-submitted",
                event_type=LedgerEventType.ORDER_SUBMITTED,
                timestamp=datetime.utcnow(),
                payload={"order": order.__dict__},
                source=order.broker,
            )
            self.event_log.append(submitted_event)
            # TODO: simulate fill and position updates

# bootstrap_max.py
from engine.trade_engine import TradeEngine
from ledger.process import LedgerProcess
from inference.server import bootstrap_inference_server
from market_data.feeds import live_or_replay_stream

def main():
    # Max’s environment assumptions:
    # - Ryzen AI 395 MAX
    # - ONNX Runtime installed
    # - Optional: PyTorch for training, not required at runtime

    ledger = LedgerProcess()
    inference_server_factory = bootstrap_inference_server

    engine = TradeEngine(
        ledger=ledger,
        inference_server_factory=inference_server_factory,
    )

    market_stream = live_or_replay_stream(symbols=["SPY", "QQQ"])
    engine.start(market_stream)

if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()

# bootstrap_max.py
from engine.trade_engine import TradeEngine
from ledger.process import LedgerProcess
from inference.server import bootstrap_inference_server
from market_data.feeds import live_or_replay_stream

def main():
    # Max’s environment assumptions:
    # - Ryzen AI 395 MAX
    # - ONNX Runtime installed
    # - Optional: PyTorch for training, not required at runtime

    ledger = LedgerProcess()
    inference_server_factory = bootstrap_inference_server

    engine = TradeEngine(
        ledger=ledger,
        inference_server_factory=inference_server_factory,
    )

    market_stream = live_or_replay_stream(symbols=["SPY", "QQQ"])
    engine.start(market_stream)

if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()

// See my thinking

def probe_node(url):
    payload = {"jsonrpc": "2.0", "id": 1, "method": "system_health", "params": []}
    try:
        r = requests.post(url, json=payload, timeout=1)
        return r.ok
    except Exception:
        return False

stateDiagram-v2
    [*] --> PQRLn_PENDING

    PQRLn_PENDING --> PQRLn_PRECHECK

    PQRLn_PRECHECK --> PQRLn_ACTIVATING: preconditions OK
    PQRLn_PRECHECK --> PQRLn_FAILED: preconditions hard-fail

    PQRLn_ACTIVATING --> PQRLn_HEALTHCHECK: activation OK
    PQRLn_ACTIVATING --> PQRLn_ROLLBACK: activation failed & rollback_on_failure

    PQRLn_HEALTHCHECK --> PQRLn_READY: all constraints satisfied
    PQRLn_HEALTHCHECK --> PQRLn_RETRYING: constraints failed & retry > 0
    PQRLn_HEALTHCHECK --> PQRLn_ROLLBACK: constraints failed & no retry, rollback_on_failure

    PQRLn_RETRYING --> PQRLn_HEALTHCHECK: retry attempt
    PQRLn_RETRYING --> PQRLn_ROLLBACK: retries exhausted

    PQRLn_ROLLBACK --> PQRLn_FAILED

    PQRLn_READY --> PQRL(n+1)_PENDING

// Cloudflare Worker pseudo-code
export default {
  async fetch(request, env, ctx) {
    // 1. Ask Substrate who should handle this
    const routerReq = {
      jsonrpc: "2.0",
      id: 1,
      method: "hcp_routeHttp",
      params: [request.url, "ROLE_VAULT_ADAPTER"]
    };
    const routerRes = await fetch(env.SUBSTRATE_RPC_URL, {
      method: "POST",
      headers: { "Content-Type": "application/json" },
      body: JSON.stringify(routerReq),
    });
    const { result } = await routerRes.json();
    const targetUrl = result.target_url; // e.g. "https://vault-node.mesh:443"

    // 2. Forward the request
    return fetch(targetUrl, request);
  }
};

// Cloudflare Worker pseudo-code
export default {
  async fetch(request, env, ctx) {
    // 1. Ask Substrate who should handle this
    const routerReq = {
      jsonrpc: "2.0",
      id: 1,
      method: "hcp_routeHttp",
      params: [request.url, "ROLE_VAULT_ADAPTER"]
    };
    const routerRes = await fetch(env.SUBSTRATE_RPC_URL, {
      method: "POST",
      headers: { "Content-Type": "application/json" },
      body: JSON.stringify(routerReq),
    });
    const { result } = await routerRes.json();
    const targetUrl = result.target_url; // e.g. "https://vault-node.mesh:443"

    // 2. Forward the request
    return fetch(targetUrl, request);
  }
};

func (tb *TraderBridge) Execute(intent TraderIntent) (TraderResult, error) {
    mut := tb.IntentToMutation(intent)

    if err := tb.SubmitProposal(mut); err != nil {
        return TraderResult{Approved: false, Rationale: err.Error()}, err
    }

    verdict := tb.AwaitConsensus(mut)

    lineageID := tb.EmitLineage(mut, verdict)

    return TraderResult{
        Approved:  verdict.Approved,
        Rationale: verdict.Rationale,
        LineageID: lineageID,
    }, nil
}
import requests

def test_vault_adapter_imagefx():
    r = requests.get(
        "http://localhost:8200/v1/secret/data/imagefx",
        headers={"X-Vault-Token": "test"},
        timeout=3,
    )
    assert r.status_code == 200
    j = r.json()
    assert "data" in j
    assert "data" in j["data"]
    assert "api_key" in j["data"]["data"]

func (tb *TraderBridge) Execute(intent TraderIntent) (TraderResult, error) {
    mut := tb.IntentToMutation(intent)

    if err := tb.SubmitProposal(mut); err != nil {
        return TraderResult{Approved: false, Rationale: err.Error()}, err
    }

    verdict := tb.AwaitConsensus(mut)

    lineageID := tb.EmitLineage(mut, verdict)

    return TraderResult{
        Approved:  verdict.Approved,
        Rationale: verdict.Rationale,
        LineageID: lineageID,
    }, nil
}

import onnxruntime as ort
from .types import InferenceRequest, InferenceResult

class InferenceServer:
    def __init__(self, model_paths):
        self.sessions = {
            name: ort.InferenceSession(path)
            for name, path in model_paths.items()
        }

    def predict(self, req: InferenceRequest) -> InferenceResult:
        session = self.sessions[req.model_name]
        inputs = {"input": req.features.astype("float32")}
        outputs = session.run(None, inputs)
        signal = float(outputs[0][0])
        confidence = float(outputs[1][0]) if len(outputs) > 1 else 1.0
        return InferenceResult(signal, confidence, {"raw": outputs})

def bootstrap_inference_server():
    return InferenceServer({
        "lstm_price_forecaster": "models/compiled/lstm_price_forecaster.onnx"
    })

async function startKeepalive(client) {
  setInterval(async () => {
    try {
      await client.send("Runtime.evaluate", {
        expression: "document.readyState"
      });
      console.log("[keepalive] session OK");
    } catch (err) {
      console.log("[keepalive] session lost, attempting recovery...");
      // reconnect logic here
    }
  }, 60000);
}

import onnxruntime as ort
from .types import InferenceRequest, InferenceResult

class InferenceServer:
    def __init__(self, model_paths):
        self.sessions = {
            name: ort.InferenceSession(path)
            for name, path in model_paths.items()
        }

    def predict(self, req: InferenceRequest) -> InferenceResult:
        session = self.sessions[req.model_name]
        inputs = {"input": req.features.astype("float32")}
        outputs = session.run(None, inputs)
        signal = float(outputs[0][0])
        confidence = float(outputs[1][0]) if len(outputs) > 1 else 1.0
        return InferenceResult(signal, confidence, {"raw": outputs})

def bootstrap_inference_server():
    return InferenceServer({
        "lstm_price_forecaster": "models/compiled/lstm_price_forecaster.onnx"
    })

func TestTraderBridge_Execute(t *testing.T) {
    tb := mockBridge()

    intent := TraderIntent{
        Symbol: "OMCL",
        Qty:    10,
        Kind:   PortfolioAdd,
    }

    result, err := tb.Execute(intent)
    if err != nil {
        t.Fatal(err)
    }

    if !result.Approved {
        t.Fatalf("expected approval, got %v", result.Rationale)
    }

    if result.LineageID == "" {
        t.Fatal("expected lineage ID")
    }
}

func TestTraderBridge_Execute(t *testing.T) {
    tb := mockBridge()

    intent := TraderIntent{
        Symbol: "OMCL",
        Qty:    10,
        Kind:   PortfolioAdd,
    }

    result, err := tb.Execute(intent)
    if err != nil {
        t.Fatal(err)
    }

    if !result.Approved {
        t.Fatalf("expected approval, got %v", result.Rationale)
    }

    if result.LineageID == "" {
        t.Fatal("expected lineage ID")
    }
}
async function startKeepalive(client) {
  setInterval(async () => {
    try {
      await client.send("Runtime.evaluate", {
        expression: "document.readyState"
      });
      console.log("[keepalive] session OK");
    } catch (err) {
      console.log("[keepalive] session lost, attempting recovery...");
      // reconnect logic here
    }

class LSTMPredictor(nn.Module):
    def __init__(self, input_dim, hidden_dim, num_layers, output_dim):
        super().__init__()
        self.lstm = nn.LSTM(input_dim, hidden_dim, num_layers, batch_first=True)
        self.fc = nn.Linear(hidden_dim, output_dim)

    def forward(self, x):
        out, _ = self.lstm(x)
        return self.fc(out[:, -1, :])

def export_to_onnx(model, path, seq_len, input_dim):
    dummy = torch.randn(1, seq_len, input_dim)
    torch.onnx.export(
        model, dummy, path,
        input_names=["input"], output_names=["signal"],
        dynamic_axes={"input": {0: "batch", 1: "seq_len"}},
        opset_version=17
    )

class LSTMPredictor(nn.Module):
    def __init__(self, input_dim, hidden_dim, num_layers, output_dim):
        super().__init__()
        self.lstm = nn.LSTM(input_dim, hidden_dim, num_layers, batch_first=True)
        self.fc = nn.Linear(hidden_dim, output_dim)

    def forward(self, x):
        out, _ = self.lstm(x)
        return self.fc(out[:, -1, :])

use sp_std::collections::btree_map::BTreeMap;
use sovereign27_runtime::Runtime;

#[test]
fn vault_api_returns_secret() {
    let key = b"imagefx".to_vec();
    let mut map = BTreeMap::new();
    map.insert(b"api_key".to_vec(), b"xyz".to_vec());

    pallet_vault_storage::VaultStorage::<Runtime>::insert(
        key.clone().try_into().unwrap(),
        map.clone()
    );

    let result = pallet_vault_storage::Pallet::<Runtime>::get_secret_raw(key).unwrap();

    assert_eq!(result.get(&b"api_key".to_vec()).unwrap(), &b"xyz".to_vec());
}

type Executor struct {
    state     State
    runlevels []RunlevelName
    current   int
    history   []RunlevelName
}

func NewExecutor(order []RunlevelName) *Executor {
    return &Executor{
        state:     StateIdle,
        runlevels: order,
        current:   -1,
        history:   []RunlevelName{},
    }
}

func (e *Executor) Boot(activate func(RunlevelName) error) error {
    e.state = StateBooting

    for i, rl := range e.runlevels {
        e.current = i

        if err := activate(rl); err != nil {
            e.state = StateFailed
            return fmt.Errorf("runlevel %s failed: %w", rl, err)
        }

        e.history = append(e.history, rl)
    }

    e.state = StateReady
    return nil
}

class TradeEngine:
    def __init__(self, ledger, inference_server_factory):
        self.ledger = ledger
        self.inference_server_factory = inference_server_factory
        self.executor = ProcessPoolExecutor(max_workers=4)

    def start(self, market_data_stream):
        self.ledger.start()

        algos = [
            mean_reversion.MeanReversionAlgo(),
            momentum.MomentumAlgo(),
            stat_arb.StatArbAlgo(),
            ml_predictor.MLPredictorAlgo(self.inference_server_factory),
        ]

        for algo in algos:
            self.executor.submit(self.run_algo_loop, algo, market_data_stream)

    def run_algo_loop(self, algo, market_data_stream):
        for bar in market_data_stream:
            orders = algo.on_bar(bar)
            for order in orders:
                self.ledger.submit_order(order)

class MLPredictorAlgo:
    def __init__(self, inference_server_factory):
        self.inference_server = inference_server_factory()
        self.name = "ml_predictor"

    def build_features(self, bar):
        return np.array([
            bar.open, bar.high, bar.low, bar.close, bar.volume
        ], dtype="float32")

    def on_bar(self, bar):
        req = InferenceRequest(
            "lstm_price_forecaster",
            self.build_features(bar),
            {"symbol": bar.symbol}
        )
        result = self.inference_server.predict(req)

        if result.signal == 0:
            return []

        side = Side.BUY if result.signal > 0 else Side.SELL

        return [
            OrderEvent(
                id=f"{self.name}-{datetime.utcnow().isoformat()}",
                source_algo=self.name,
                broker="schwab_sim",
                symbol=bar.symbol,
                side=side,
                qty=10,
                order_type=OrderType.MARKET,
                limit_price=None,
                timestamp=datetime.utcnow(),
                metadata={"signal": result.signal}
            )
        ]

// api_trader.go
package api

import (
    "encoding/json"
    "net/http"

    "your/module/swarm"
)

type ExecuteRequest struct {
    Symbol    string  `json:"symbol"`
    Qty       float64 `json:"qty"`
    CostBasis float64 `json:"cost_basis"`
    Kind      string  `json:"kind"`
}

type ExecuteResponse struct {
    Approved  bool   `json:"approved"`
    Rationale string `json:"rationale"`
    LineageID string `json:"lineage_id"`
}

type TraderAPI struct {
    Bridge *swarm.TraderBridge
}

func (api *TraderAPI) HandleExecute(w http.ResponseWriter, r *http.Request) {
    var req ExecuteRequest
    if err := json.NewDecoder(r.Body).Decode(&req); err != nil {
        http.Error(w, err.Error(), http.StatusBadRequest)
        return
    }

    intent := swarm.TraderIntent{
        Symbol:    req.Symbol,
        Qty:       req.Qty,
        CostBasis: req.CostBasis,
        Kind:      swarm.FinancialMutationKind(req.Kind),
    }

    result, err := api.Bridge.Execute(intent)
    if err != nil {
        // still return structured response
        resp := ExecuteResponse{
            Approved:  false,
            Rationale: err.Error(),
            LineageID: "",
        }
        w.Header().Set("Content-Type", "application/json")
        json.NewEncoder(w).Encode(resp)
        return
    }

    resp := ExecuteResponse{
        Approved:  result.Approved,
        Rationale: result.Rationale,
        LineageID: result.LineageID,
    }

    w.Header().Set("Content-Type", "application/json")
    json.NewEncoder(w).Encode(resp)
}

class LedgerProcess:
    def __init__(self):
        self.order_queue = Queue()
        self.event_log = []

    def start(self):
        self.proc = Process(target=self._run)
        self.proc.start()

    def submit_order(self, order):
        self.order_queue.put(order)

    def _run(self):
        while True:
            order = self.order_queue.get()

            event = LedgerEvent(
                id=f"ledger-{order.id}-submitted",
                event_type=LedgerEventType.ORDER_SUBMITTED,
                timestamp=datetime.utcnow(),
                payload={"order": order.__dict__},
                source=order.broker,
            )
            self.event_log.append(event)
            # TODO: simulate fills, slippage, latency, P&L

2001:db8:27:7f3a::/64
import os
import random
import dns.resolver
import requests

DEFAULT_URL = os.getenv("SUBSTRATE_RPC_URL", "http://127.0.0.1:9933")

def probe(url):
    payload = {
        "jsonrpc": "2.0",
        "id": 1,
        "method": "system_health",
        "params": []
    }
    try:
        r = requests.post(url, json=payload, timeout=1)
        return r.ok
    except Exception:
        return False

def discover_node():
    try:
        answers = dns.resolver.resolve("_substrate._tcp.mesh.pqr.info", "SRV")
        candidates = []
        for rdata in answers:
            host = str(rdata.target).rstrip(".")
            port = rdata.port
            url = f"http://{host}:{port}"
            if probe(url):
                candidates.append(url)
        if candidates:
            return random.choice(candidates)
        return DEFAULT_URL
    except Exception:
        return DEFAULT_URL

def aggregate_depth(symbol, snapshots: list[SourceDepthSnapshot]) -> AggregatedDepth:
    ts = max(s.timestamp for s in snapshots)

    per_source_spread = {}
    per_source_liquidity = {}
    for s in snapshots:
        best_bid = s.bids[0].price if s.bids else None
        best_ask = s.asks[0].price if s.asks else None
        if best_bid is not None and best_ask is not None:
            per_source_spread[s.source] = best_ask - best_bid
        total_depth = sum(l.size for l in s.bids + s.asks)
        per_source_liquidity[s.source] = total_depth

    # consolidated best bid/ask
    all_bids = [s.bids[0].price for s in snapshots if s.bids]
    all_asks = [s.asks[0].price for s in snapshots if s.asks]
    consolidated_best_bid = max(all_bids) if all_bids else None
    consolidated_best_ask = min(all_asks) if all_asks else None
    consolidated_spread = (
        consolidated_best_ask - consolidated_best_bid
        if consolidated_best_bid is not None and consolidated_best_ask is not None
        else None
    )

    # cross-source widespread
    cross_source_widespread = {}
    for a in snapshots:
        for b in snapshots:
            if a.source == b.source:
                continue
            if a.bids and b.asks:
                cross_source_widespread[(a.source, b.source)] = b.asks[0].price - a.bids[0].price

    return AggregatedDepth(
        symbol=symbol,
        timestamp=ts,
        sources=snapshots,
        consolidated_best_bid=consolidated_best_bid,
        consolidated_best_ask=consolidated_best_ask,
        consolidated_spread=consolidated_spread,
        per_source_spread=per_source_spread,
        per_source_liquidity=per_source_liquidity,
        cross_source_widespread=cross_source_widespread,
    )

// app/api/trader/execute/route.ts (Next.js 13+)
import { NextRequest, NextResponse } from 'next/server';

export async function POST(req: NextRequest) {
  const body = await req.json();

  const res = await fetch('http://localhost:8080/api/trader/execute', {
    method: 'POST',
    headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
    body: JSON.stringify(body),
  });

  const data = await res.json();
  return NextResponse.json(data);
}

def aggregate_depth(symbol, snapshots: list[SourceDepthSnapshot]) -> AggregatedDepth:
    ts = max(s.timestamp for s in snapshots)

    per_source_spread = {}
    per_source_liquidity = {}
    for s in snapshots:
        best_bid = s.bids[0].price if s.bids else None
        best_ask = s.asks[0].price if s.asks else None
        if best_bid is not None and best_ask is not None:
            per_source_spread[s.source] = best_ask - best_bid
        total_depth = sum(l.size for l in s.bids + s.asks)
        per_source_liquidity[s.source] = total_depth

    # consolidated best bid/ask
    all_bids = [s.bids[0].price for s in snapshots if s.bids]
    all_asks = [s.asks[0].price for s in snapshots if s.asks]
    consolidated_best_bid = max(all_bids) if all_bids else None
    consolidated_best_ask = min(all_asks) if all_asks else None
    consolidated_spread = (
        consolidated_best_ask - consolidated_best_bid
        if consolidated_best_bid is not None and consolidated_best_ask is not None
        else None
    )

    # cross-source widespread
    cross_source_widespread = {}
    for a in snapshots:
        for b in snapshots:
            if a.source == b.source:
                continue
            if a.bids and b.asks:
                cross_source_widespread[(a.source, b.source)] = b.asks[0].price - a.bids[0].price

    return AggregatedDepth(
        symbol=symbol,
        timestamp=ts,
        sources=snapshots,
        consolidated_best_bid=consolidated_best_bid,
        consolidated_best_ask=consolidated_best_ask,
        consolidated_spread=consolidated_spread,
        per_source_spread=per_source_spread,
        per_source_liquidity=per_source_liquidity,
        cross_source_widespread=cross_source_widespread,
    )

// components/OrderTicket.tsx
import { useState } from 'react';

type ExecuteResponse = {
  approved: boolean;
  rationale: string;
  lineage_id: string;
};

export function OrderTicket() {
  const [symbol, setSymbol] = useState('OMCL');
  const [qty, setQty] = useState(10);
  const [costBasis, setCostBasis] = useState(44.92);
  const [kind, setKind] = useState<'portfolio_add' | 'portfolio_remove'>('portfolio_add');

  const [result, setResult] = useState<ExecuteResponse | null>(null);
  const [loading, setLoading] = useState(false);

  async function submit() {
    setLoading(true);
    try {
      const res = await fetch('/api/trader/execute', {
        method: 'POST',
        headers: { 'Content-Type': 'application/json' },
        body: JSON.stringify({ symbol, qty, cost_basis: costBasis, kind }),
      });
      const data: ExecuteResponse = await res.json();
      setResult(data);
    } finally {
      setLoading(false);
    }
  }

  return (
    <div>
      {/* inputs for symbol, qty, costBasis, kind */}
      <button onClick={submit} disabled={loading}>
        Submit Buy Order
      </button>

      {result && (
        <div>
          <div>Approved: {String(result.approved)}</div>
          <div>Rationale: {result.rationale}</div>
          <div>Lineage ID: {result.lineage_id}</div>
        </div>
      )}
    </div>
  );
}

    ACTIVATING --> ROLLBACK: activation failed & rollback_on_failure

    HEALTHCHECK --> READY: all constraints satisfied
    HEALTHCHECK --> RETRYING: constraints failed & retry > 0
    HEALTHCHECK --> ROLLBACK: constraints failed & no retry

    RETRYING --> HEALTHCHECK: retry attempt
    RETRYING --> ROLLBACK: retries exhausted

    ROLLBACK --> FAILED

    READY --> NEXT

// See my thinking

latest = {
    "schwab": None,
    "alpaca": None,
    "source_c": None,
}

def aggregator_loop():
    while True:
        # FIFO consumption from each queue
        if not schwab_depth_q.empty():
            latest["schwab"] = schwab_depth_q.get()

        if not alpaca_depth_q.empty():
            latest["alpaca"] = alpaca_depth_q.get()

        if not source_c_depth_q.empty():
            latest["source_c"] = source_c_depth_q.get()

        # When we have at least one snapshot, aggregate
        snapshots = [s for s in latest.values() if s is not None]
        if snapshots:
            agg = aggregate_depth(symbol="*", snapshots=snapshots)
            publish_aggregated_depth(agg)
type Runlevel struct {
    ID          int
    Name        string
    Description string

    Preconditions map[string]string
    Activate      ActivateBlock
    Health        HealthBlock
    Constraints   ConstraintBlock
    Advance       AdvanceBlock
}

type ActivateBlock struct {
    Command    string
    Container  string
    DependsOn  []string
}

type HealthBlock struct {
    GRPCPort         int
    ContainerRunning bool
    CheckEndpoint    string
    TimeoutMS        int
}

type ConstraintBlock struct {
    RequireAll        bool
    Retry             int
    RetryBackoffMS    int
    RollbackOnFailure bool
    RollbackCommand   string
    EscalateTo        string
}

type AdvanceBlock struct {
    Next int
}

func RunProposalEngine(ctx context.Context, bio *Biographer) {
    ticker := time.NewTicker(5 * time.Minute)
    defer ticker.Stop()

    for {
        select {
        case <-ctx.Done():
            return
        case <-ticker.C:
            insights := loadRecentInsights()
            for _, ins := range insights {
                proposals := GenerateProposals(ins)
                for _, p := range proposals {
                    writeProposalMarkdown(p)
                    emitProposalEvent(ctx, bio, p)
                }
            }
        }
    }
}
// See my thinking

func RunProposalEngine(ctx context.Context, bio *Biographer) {
    ticker := time.NewTicker(5 * time.Minute)
    defer ticker.Stop()

    for {
        select {
        case <-ctx.Done():
            return
        case <-ticker.C:
            insights := loadRecentInsights()
            for _, ins := range insights {
                proposals := GenerateProposals(ins)
                for _, p := range proposals {
                    writeProposalMarkdown(p)
                    emitProposalEvent(ctx, bio, p)
                }
            }
        }
    }
}

func (e *Executor) Run() error {
    for _, rl := range e.runlevels {
        if err := e.runRunlevel(rl); err != nil {
            return err
        }
    }
    return nil
}

func (e *Executor) runRunlevel(rl Runlevel) error {
    state := PENDING

    // PRECHECK
    if err := e.checkPreconditions(rl); err != nil {
        return e.fail(rl, PRECHECK, err)
    }

    // ACTIVATING
    if err := e.activate(rl); err != nil {
        if rl.Constraints.RollbackOnFailure {
            return e.rollback(rl, err)
        }
        return e.fail(rl, ACTIVATING, err)
    }

    // HEALTHCHECK + CONSTRAINTS
    for attempt := 0; attempt <= rl.Constraints.Retry; attempt++ {
        if err := e.checkHealth(rl); err != nil {
            if attempt < rl.Constraints.Retry {
                time.Sleep(time.Duration(rl.Constraints.RetryBackoffMS) * time.Millisecond)
                continue
            }
            return e.rollback(rl, err)
        }

        // READY
        return e.ready(rl)
    }

    return nil
}

func (e *Executor) runRunlevel(rl Runlevel) error {
    state := PENDING

    // PRECHECK
    if err := e.checkPreconditions(rl); err != nil {
        return e.fail(rl, PRECHECK, err)
    }

    // ACTIVATING
    if err := e.activate(rl); err != nil {
        if rl.Constraints.RollbackOnFailure {
            return e.rollback(rl, err)
        }
        return e.fail(rl, ACTIVATING, err)
    }

    // HEALTHCHECK + CONSTRAINTS
    for attempt := 0; attempt <= rl.Constraints.Retry; attempt++ {
        if err := e.checkHealth(rl); err != nil {
            if attempt < rl.Constraints.Retry {
                time.Sleep(time.Duration(rl.Constraints.RetryBackoffMS) * time.Millisecond)
                continue
            }
            return e.rollback(rl, err)
        }

        // READY
        return e.ready(rl)
    }

    return nil
}

func (e *Executor) runRunlevel(rl Runlevel) error {
    state := PENDING

    // PRECHECK
    if err := e.checkPreconditions(rl); err != nil {
        return e.fail(rl, PRECHECK, err)
    }

    // ACTIVATING
    if err := e.activate(rl); err != nil {
        if rl.Constraints.RollbackOnFailure {
            return e.rollback(rl, err)
        }
        return e.fail(rl, ACTIVATING, err)
    }

    // HEALTHCHECK + CONSTRAINTS
    for attempt := 0; attempt <= rl.Constraints.Retry; attempt++ {
        if err := e.checkHealth(rl); err != nil {
            if attempt < rl.Constraints.Retry {
                time.Sleep(time.Duration(rl.Constraints.RetryBackoffMS) * time.Millisecond)
                continue
            }
            return e.rollback(rl, err)
        }

        // READY
        return e.ready(rl)
    }

    return nil
}

package netheal

import (
    "context"
    "errors"
    "net"
    "time"
)

type Status string

const (
    StatusHealthy   Status = "healthy"
    StatusDegraded  Status = "degraded"
    StatusUnhealthy Status = "unhealthy"
)

type Config struct {
    Addr          string        // host:port
    DialTimeout   time.Duration // per attempt
    MaxRetries    int
    RetryBackoff  time.Duration
    HealthInterval time.Duration
}

type Core struct {
    cfg    Config
    status Status
}

TIME_MACHINE/microstructure
TIME_MACHINE/microstructure
// See my thinking

func (c *Core) Start(ctx context.Context) {
    ticker := time.NewTicker(c.cfg.HealthInterval)
    defer ticker.Stop()

    for {
        select {
        case <-ctx.Done():
            return
        case <-ticker.C:
            if err := c.checkAndHeal(); err != nil {
                c.status = StatusUnhealthy
            }
        }
    }
}

func (c *Core) checkAndHeal() error {
    if err := c.checkOnce(); err == nil {
        c.status = StatusHealthy
        return nil
    }

    // attempt self‑healing
    for i := 0; i < c.cfg.MaxRetries; i++ {
        time.Sleep(c.cfg.RetryBackoff)
        if err := c.checkOnce(); err == nil {
            c.status = StatusDegraded
            return nil
        }
    }

    return errors.New("self‑healing failed")
}

func (c *Core) checkOnce() error {
    dialCtx, cancel := context.WithTimeout(context.Background(), c.cfg.DialTimeout)
    defer cancel()

    conn, err := net.DialTimeout("tcp", c.cfg.Addr, c.cfg.DialTimeout)
    if err != nil {
        return err
    }
    _ = conn.Close()
    return nil
}

# See my thinking

import random
import numpy as np
from datetime import time

def sample_distribution(dist, rng=random):
    t = dist["type"]
    if t == "fixed":
        return dist["ms"]
    if t == "uniform":
        return rng.uniform(dist["min_ms"], dist["max_ms"])
    if t == "normal":
        val = rng.gauss(dist["mean_ms"], dist["std_ms"])
        if "min_ms" in dist:
            val = max(val, dist["min_ms"])
        if "max_ms" in dist:
            val = min(val, dist["max_ms"])
        return val
    if t == "exponential":
        val = rng.expovariate(dist["lambda"])
        if "min_ms" in dist:
            val = max(val, dist["min_ms"])
        if "max_ms" in dist:
            val = min(val, dist["max_ms"])
        return val
    raise ValueError(f"Unknown distribution type: {t}")

def time_in_window(ts: datetime, window: dict) -> bool:
    if not window:
        return True
    start = time.fromisoformat(window["start"])
    end = time.fromisoformat(window["end"])
    return start <= ts.time() <= end

def compute_latency_ms(order, event_type, profiles, context) -> float:
    """
    context: {
      "current_load": float,  # orders/sec
      "spread_bps": float,
      "bar_volume": float
    }
    """
    applicable_rules = []
    for profile in profiles["profiles"]:
        for rule in profile["rules"]:
            scope = rule["scope"]
            symbols = scope.get("symbols", ["*"])
            event_types = scope.get("event_types", [])
            window = scope.get("time_window")

            symbol_match = ("*" in symbols) or (order.symbol in symbols)
            event_match = (not event_types) or (event_type in event_types)
            time_match = time_in_window(order.timestamp, window) if window else True

            if symbol_match and event_match and time_match:
                applicable_rules.append(rule)

    if not applicable_rules:
        return 0.0

    # For now: take the most specific rule (last match wins)
    rule = applicable_rules[-1]
    base = sample_distribution(rule["base_distribution"])

    total_delta = 0.0
    for mod in rule.get("modifiers", []):
        if mod["type"] == "by_volume":
            vol = context.get("bar_volume", 0)
            for th in mod["thresholds"]:
                if vol <= th["max_volume"]:
                    total_delta += th["delta_ms"]
                    break
        elif mod["type"] == "by_load":
            load = context.get("current_load", 0)
            for th in mod["thresholds"]:
                if load <= th["max"]:
                    total_delta += th["delta_ms"]
                    break
        elif mod["type"] == "by_spread":
            spread = context.get("spread_bps", 0)
            for th in mod["thresholds"]:
                if spread <= th["max_spread_bps"]:
                    total_delta += th["delta_ms"]
                    break

    return base + total_delta

from datetime import time

def sample_distribution(dist, rng=random):

        return dist["ms"]
    if t == "uniform":
        return rng.uniform(dist["min_ms"], dist["max_ms"])
    if t == "normal":
        val = rng.gauss(dist["mean_ms"], dist["std_ms"])
        if "min_ms" in dist:
            val = max(val, dist["min_ms"])
        if "max_ms" in dist:
            val = min(val, dist["max_ms"])
        return val
    if t == "exponential":
        val = rng.expovariate(dist["lambda"])
        if "min_ms" in dist:
            val = max(val, dist["min_ms"])
        if "max_ms" in dist:
            val = min(val, dist["max_ms"])
        return val
    raise ValueError(f"Unknown distribution type: {t}")

    if not window:
        return True
    start = time.fromisoformat(window["start"])
    end = time.fromisoformat(window["end"])
    return start <= ts.time() <= end

def compute_latency_ms(order, event_type, profiles, context) -> float:
    """
    context: {
      "current_load": float,  # orders/sec
      "spread_bps": float,
      "bar_volume": float
    }
    """

    for profile in profiles["profiles"]:
        for rule in profile["rules"]:
            scope = rule["scope"]
            symbols = scope.get("symbols", ["*"])
            event_types = scope.get("event_types", [])
            window = scope.get("time_window")

            symbol_match = ("*" in symbols) or (order.symbol in symbols)
            event_match = (not event_types) or (event_type in event_types)
            time_match = time_in_window(order.timestamp, window) if window else True

            if symbol_match and event_match and time_match:
                applicable_rules.append(rule)

    if not applicable_rules:
        return 0.0

    # For now: take the most specific rule (last match wins)
    rule = applicable_rules[-1]
    base = sample_distribution(rule["base_distribution"])

    total_delta = 0.0

            vol = context.get("bar_volume", 0)
            for th in mod["thresholds"]:
                if vol <= th["max_volume"]:
                    total_delta += th["delta_ms"]
                    break
        elif mod["type"] == "by_load":
            load = context.get("current_load", 0)
            for th in mod["thresholds"]:
                if load <= th["max"]:
                    total_delta += th["delta_ms"]
                    break
        elif mod["type"] == "by_spread":
            spread = context.get("spread_bps", 0)
            for th in mod["thresholds"]:
                if spread <= th["max_spread_bps"]:
                    total_delta += th["delta_ms"]
                    break

    return base + total_delta
