def _run(self):
    while True:
        order: OrderEvent = self.order_queue.get()

        context = {
            "current_load": self._estimate_load(),
            "spread_bps": self._get_spread(order.symbol),
            "bar_volume": self._get_last_bar_volume(order.symbol),
        }

        latency_ms = compute_latency_ms(
            order,
            LedgerEventType.ORDER_SUBMITTED.value,
            self.latency_profiles,
            context
        )

        time.sleep(latency_ms / 1000.0)

        submitted_event = LedgerEvent(
            id=f"ledger-{order.id}-submitted",
            event_type=LedgerEventType.ORDER_SUBMITTED,
            timestamp=datetime.utcnow(),
            payload={"order": order.__dict__, "latency_ms": latency_ms},
            source=order.broker,
        )
        self.event_log.append(submitted_event)

        # Similar logic for ORDER_FILLED with its own latency profile

def _run(self):
    while True:
        order: OrderEvent = self.order_queue.get()

        context = {
            "current_load": self._estimate_load(),
            "spread_bps": self._get_spread(order.symbol),
            "bar_volume": self._get_last_bar_volume(order.symbol),
        }

        latency_ms = compute_latency_ms(
            order,
            LedgerEventType.ORDER_SUBMITTED.value,
            self.latency_profiles,
            context
        )

        time.sleep(latency_ms / 1000.0)

        submitted_event = LedgerEvent(
            id=f"ledger-{order.id}-submitted",
            event_type=LedgerEventType.ORDER_SUBMITTED,
            timestamp=datetime.utcnow(),
            payload={"order": order.__dict__, "latency_ms": latency_ms},
            source=order.broker,
        )
        self.event_log.append(submitted_event)

package timemachine

import (
    "encoding/json"
    "fmt"
    "os"
    "path/filepath"
    "strings"
    "time"
)

type MicrostructureEvent map[string]any

func formatTimestampForFilename(ts time.Time) string {
    s := ts.UTC().Format(time.RFC3339Nano)
    s = strings.ReplaceAll(s, ":", "-")
    return s
}

func microstructurePath(root string, ts time.Time) string {
    return filepath.Join(
        root,
        "microstructure",
        fmt.Sprintf("%04d", ts.Year()),
        fmt.Sprintf("%02d", ts.Month()),
        fmt.Sprintf("%02d", ts.Day()),
        fmt.Sprintf("%02d", ts.Hour()),
        fmt.Sprintf("%02d", ts.Minute()),
        formatTimestampForFilename(ts)+".json",
    )
}

func WriteMicrostructureEvent(root string, ts time.Time, event MicrostructureEvent) error {
    path := microstructurePath(root, ts)
    if err := os.MkdirAll(filepath.Dir(path), 0o755); err != nil {
        return fmt.Errorf("mkdir: %w", err)
    }
    b, err := json.MarshalIndent(event, "", "  ")
    if err != nil {
        return fmt.Errorf("marshal: %w", err)
    }
    if err := os.WriteFile(path, b, 0o644); err != nil {
        return fmt.Errorf("write: %w", err)
    }
    return nil
}

        root,
        "microstructure",
        fmt.Sprintf("%04d", ts.Year()),
        fmt.Sprintf("%02d", ts.Month()),
        fmt.Sprintf("%02d", ts.Day()),
        fmt.Sprintf("%02d", ts.Hour()),
        fmt.Sprintf("%02d", ts.Minute()),
        formatTimestampForFilename(ts)+".json",
    )

    path := microstructurePath(root, ts)
    if err := os.MkdirAll(filepath.Dir(path), 0o755); err != nil {
        return fmt.Errorf("mkdir: %w", err)

        return fmt.Errorf("marshal: %w", err)
    }
    if err := os.WriteFile(path, b, 0o644); err != nil {
        return fmt.Errorf("write: %w", err)
    }
    return nil

const fs = require('fs');
const path = require('path');

function formatTimestampForFilename(ts) {
  return ts.toISOString().replace(/:/g, '-');
}

function microstructurePath(root, ts) {
  return path.join(
    root,
    'microstructure',
    ts.getUTCFullYear().toString().padStart(4, '0'),
    (ts.getUTCMonth() + 1).toString().padStart(2, '0'),
    ts.getUTCDate().toString().padStart(2, '0'),
    ts.getUTCHours().toString().padStart(2, '0'),
    ts.getUTCMinutes().toString().padStart(2, '0'),
    `${formatTimestampForFilename(ts)}.json`
  );
}

function writeMicrostructureEvent(root, event) {
  const ts = new Date(event.timestamp || new Date().toISOString());
  const filePath = microstructurePath(root, ts);
  fs.mkdirSync(path.dirname(filePath), { recursive: true });
  fs.writeFileSync(filePath, JSON.stringify(event, null, 2), 'utf8');
}

module.exports = { writeMicrostructureEvent };

import json
import os
from datetime import datetime, timezone

def format_timestamp_for_filename(ts: datetime) -> str:
    return ts.astimezone(timezone.utc).isoformat().replace(":", "-")

def microstructure_path(root: str, ts: datetime) -> str:
    ts = ts.astimezone(timezone.utc)
    return os.path.join(
        root,
        "microstructure",
        f"{ts.year:04d}",
        f"{ts.month:02d}",
        f"{ts.day:02d}",
        f"{ts.hour:02d}",
        f"{ts.minute:02d}",
        f"{format_timestamp_for_filename(ts)}.json",
    )

def write_microstructure_event(root: str, ts: datetime, event: dict) -> None:
    path = microstructure_path(root, ts)
    os.makedirs(os.path.dirname(path), exist_ok=True)
    with open(path, "w", encoding="utf-8") as f:
        json.dump(event, f, indent=2)

import json
import os
from datetime import datetime, timezone

def format_timestamp_for_filename(ts: datetime) -> str:
    return ts.astimezone(timezone.utc).isoformat().replace(":", "-")

def microstructure_path(root: str, ts: datetime) -> str:
    ts = ts.astimezone(timezone.utc)
    return os.path.join(
        root,
        "microstructure",
        f"{ts.year:04d}",
        f"{ts.month:02d}",
        f"{ts.day:02d}",
        f"{ts.hour:02d}",
        f"{ts.minute:02d}",
        f"{format_timestamp_for_filename(ts)}.json",
    )

def write_microstructure_event(root: str, ts: datetime, event: dict) -> None:
    path = microstructure_path(root, ts)
    os.makedirs(os.path.dirname(path), exist_ok=True)
    with open(path, "w", encoding="utf-8") as f:
        json.dump(event, f, indent=2)

import time
from dataclasses import dataclass

@dataclass
class LatencyResult:
    name: str
    start_ts: float
    end_ts: float
    duration_ms: float
    metadata: dict

def measure_latency(name: str, func, *args, **kwargs) -> LatencyResult:
    start = time.perf_counter()
    result = func(*args, **kwargs)
    end = time.perf_counter()
    return LatencyResult(
        name=name,
        start_ts=start,
        end_ts=end,
        duration_ms=(end - start) * 1000.0,
        metadata={"result": result}
    )

from datetime import datetime, timezone

def publish_aggregated_depth(agg, positions, market_data, signals, heatmap, latency):
    ts = datetime.now(timezone.utc)
    event = {
        "timestamp": ts.isoformat(),
        "positions": positions,
        "depth": agg.__dict__,
        "market_data": market_data,
        "signals": signals,
        "capital_heatmap": heatmap,
        "latency": latency,
    }
    write_microstructure_event("TIME_MACHINE", ts, event)

import time
from dataclasses import dataclass

@dataclass
class LatencyResult:
    name: str
    start_ts: float
    end_ts: float
    duration_ms: float
    metadata: dict

def measure_latency(name: str, func, *args, **kwargs) -> LatencyResult:
    start = time.perf_counter()
    result = func(*args, **kwargs)
    end = time.perf_counter()
    return LatencyResult(
        name=name,
        start_ts=start,
        end_ts=end,
        duration_ms=(end - start) * 1000.0,
        metadata={"result": result}
    )

import requests
import json
from latency.measure import measure_latency

def fetch_price(symbol: str):
    r = requests.get(f"http://localhost:8000/price?symbol={symbol}")
    return r.json()

def parse_price(data):
    return {
        "symbol": data["symbol"],
        "price": float(data["price"]),
        "timestamp": data["timestamp"]
    }

# Measure HTTP latency
http_latency = measure_latency("http_price_fetch", fetch_price, "SPY")

# Measure JSON parse latency
parse_latency = measure_latency("json_parse", parse_price, http_latency.metadata["result"])

print(http_latency)
print(parse_latency)

impl vault_runtime_api::VaultApi<Block> for Runtime {
    fn vault_get_secret(key: Vec<u8>) -> Option<BTreeMap<Vec<u8>, Vec<u8>>> {
        pallet_vault_storage::Pallet::<Runtime>::get_secret_raw(key)
    }

    fn vault_get_shard(key: Vec<u8>, node_id: Vec<u8>) -> Option<Vec<u8>> {
        pallet_vault_storage::Pallet::<Runtime>::get_shard_raw(key, node_id)
    }

    fn vault_get_sharded_descriptor(
        key: Vec<u8>
    ) -> Option<(u8, u8, Vec<Vec<u8>>)> {
        pallet_vault_storage::Pallet::<Runtime>::get_sharded_descriptor_raw(key)
    }
}

package main

import (
    "encoding/json"
    "flag"
    "fmt"
    "os"
    "path/filepath"
)

func main() {
    root := flag.String("root", "TIME_MACHINE", "Time Machine root")
    date := flag.String("date", "", "Date YYYY-MM-DD")
    flag.Parse()

    if *date == "" {
        fmt.Println("date required")
        os.Exit(1)
    }

    // e.g. TIME_MACHINE/microstructure/2026/07/20
    var year, month, day string
    fmt.Sscanf(*date, "%4s-%2s-%2s", &year, &month, &day)
    base := filepath.Join(*root, "microstructure", year, month, day)

    err := filepath.Walk(base, func(path string, info os.FileInfo, err error) error {
        if err != nil || info.IsDir() || filepath.Ext(path) != ".json" {
            return nil
        }
        b, err := os.ReadFile(path)
        if err != nil {
            return err
        }
        var event map[string]any
        if err := json.Unmarshal(b, &event); err != nil {
            return err
        }
        // Here you can feed event into a replay engine, print summary, etc.
        fmt.Println("Event:", event["timestamp"])
        return nil
    })
    if err != nil {
        fmt.Println("replay error:", err)
        os.Exit(1)
    }
}

impl<T: Config> Pallet<T> {
    // Existing:
    pub fn get_secret_raw(key: Vec<u8>) -> Option<BTreeMap<Vec<u8>, Vec<u8>>> {
        let bounded_key =
            BoundedVec::<u8, T::MaxKeyLength>::try_from(key.clone()).ok()?;
        let stored = VaultStorage::<T>::get(bounded_key)?;
        let mut out = BTreeMap::new();
        for (k, v) in stored.into_iter() {
            out.insert(k.into_inner(), v.into_inner());
        }
        Some(out)
    }

    // NEW: get a single shard by key + node_id
    pub fn get_shard_raw(key: Vec<u8>, node_id: Vec<u8>) -> Option<Vec<u8>> {
        let bounded_key =
            BoundedVec::<u8, T::MaxKeyLength>::try_from(key.clone()).ok()?;
        let bounded_node =
            BoundedVec::<u8, T::MaxKeyLength>::try_from(node_id.clone()).ok()?;

        // shard key convention: "shard::<key>::<node_id>"
        let mut shard_key = bounded_key.clone().into_inner();
        shard_key.extend_from_slice(b"::");
        shard_key.extend_from_slice(&bounded_node);

        let bounded_shard_key =
            BoundedVec::<u8, T::MaxKeyLength>::try_from(shard_key).ok()?;

        let stored = VaultStorage::<T>::get(bounded_shard_key)?;
        // assume single-entry map: { "shard": <bytes> }
        for (_k, v) in stored.into_iter() {
            return Some(v.into_inner());
        }
        None
    }

    // NEW: get sharded descriptor (total, threshold, node_ids)
    pub fn get_sharded_descriptor_raw(
        key: Vec<u8>
    ) -> Option<(u8, u8, Vec<Vec<u8>>)> {
        let bounded_key =
            BoundedVec::<u8, T::MaxKeyLength>::try_from(key.clone()).ok()?;
        let desc = ShardedSecrets::<T>::get(bounded_key)?;
        let node_ids = desc
            .node_ids
            .into_iter()
            .map(|n| n.into_inner())
            .collect::<Vec<_>>();
        Some((desc.total_shards, desc.threshold, node_ids))
    }
}

package main

import (
    "encoding/json"
    "flag"
    "fmt"
    "os"
    "path/filepath"
)

func main() {
    root := flag.String("root", "TIME_MACHINE", "Time Machine root")
    date := flag.String("date", "", "Date YYYY-MM-DD")
    flag.Parse()

    if *date == "" {
        fmt.Println("date required")
        os.Exit(1)
    }

    // e.g. TIME_MACHINE/microstructure/2026/07/20
    var year, month, day string
    fmt.Sscanf(*date, "%4s-%2s-%2s", &year, &month, &day)
    base := filepath.Join(*root, "microstructure", year, month, day)

    err := filepath.Walk(base, func(path string, info os.FileInfo, err error) error {
        if err != nil || info.IsDir() || filepath.Ext(path) != ".json" {
            return nil
        }
        b, err := os.ReadFile(path)
        if err != nil {
            return err
        }
        var event map[string]any
        if err := json.Unmarshal(b, &event); err != nil {
            return err
        }
        // Here you can feed event into a replay engine, print summary, etc.
        fmt.Println("Event:", event["timestamp"])
        return nil
    })
    if err != nil {
        fmt.Println("replay error:", err)
        os.Exit(1)
    }
}

#[rpc]
pub trait VaultRpc {
    #[rpc(name = "vault_getSecret")]
    fn vault_getSecret(&self, key: String) -> Result<std::collections::HashMap<String, String>>;

    // NEW: get a single shard
    #[rpc(name = "vault_getShard")]
    fn vault_getShard(&self, key: String, node_id: String) -> Result<String>;

    // NEW: get sharded descriptor
    #[rpc(name = "vault_getShardedDescriptor")]
    fn vault_getShardedDescriptor(
        &self,
        key: String
    ) -> Result<(u8, u8, Vec<String>)>;
}

@dataclass
class PositionState:
    symbol: str
    side: str  # "long" / "short"
    qty: float
    entry_price: float
    current_price: float
    unrealized_pnl_pct: float
    holding_days: float

@dataclass
class SignalSummary:
    symbol: str
    liquidation_risk_score: float
    wave_continuation_score: float
    snipe_opportunity_score: float
    details: dict

class PositionAnalyzer:
    def __init__(self, signal_sources):
        self.signal_sources = signal_sources  # ml, momentum, etc.

    def analyze_position(self, pos: PositionState, regime, market_context) -> SignalSummary:
        ml_signal = self.signal_sources["ml"].get_signal(pos.symbol)
        momentum_signal = self.signal_sources["momentum"].get_signal(pos.symbol)
        mr_signal = self.signal_sources["mean_reversion"].get_signal(pos.symbol)
        stat_arb_signal = self.signal_sources["stat_arb"].get_signal(pos.symbol)

        liquidation_risk = 0.0
        wave_continuation = 0.0
        snipe_opportunity = 0.0

        # Liquidation risk
        if pos.unrealized_pnl_pct < -market_context["max_dd_pct"]:
            liquidation_risk += 0.4
        if regime.trend == "down" and pos.side == "long":
            liquidation_risk += 0.3
        if ml_signal < 0 and momentum_signal < 0:
            liquidation_risk += 0.3

        # Wave continuation
        if regime.trend == "up" and pos.side == "long":
            wave_continuation += 0.4
        if ml_signal > 0 and momentum_signal > 0:
            wave_continuation += 0.4
        if market_context["volatility_in_range"]:
            wave_continuation += 0.2

        # Snipe opportunity (for symbols you don't hold or small size)
        if pos.qty == 0 or pos.qty < market_context["min_size_for_full"]:
            if ml_signal > market_context["ml_threshold"] and momentum_signal > market_context["mom_threshold"]:
                snipe_opportunity += 0.5
            if mr_signal < -market_context["mr_z_threshold"]:
                snipe_opportunity += 0.3
            if market_context["spread_bps"] < market_context["max_spread_bps"]:
                snipe_opportunity += 0.2

        return SignalSummary(
            symbol=pos.symbol,
            liquidation_risk_score=liquidation_risk,
            wave_continuation_score=wave_continuation,
            snipe_opportunity_score=snipe_opportunity,
            details={
                "ml_signal": ml_signal,
                "momentum_signal": momentum_signal,
                "mr_signal": mr_signal,
                "stat_arb_signal": stat_arb_signal,
                "regime": regime.__dict__,
                "position": pos.__dict__,
            }
        )

@dataclass
class SignalSummary:
    symbol: str
    liquidation_risk_score: float
    wave_continuation_score: float
    snipe_opportunity_score: float
    details: dict

class PositionAnalyzer:
    def __init__(self, signal_sources):

Lease
impl<C, P> VaultRpc for VaultRpcHandler<C, P>
where
    C: ProvideRuntimeApi<Block> + HeaderBackend<Block>,
    C::Api: vault_runtime_api::VaultApi<Block>,
{
    fn vault_getSecret(&self, key: String) -> Result<std::collections::HashMap<String, String>> {
        let api = self.client.runtime_api();
        let result = api.vault_get_secret(key.clone().into_bytes());

        let mut out = std::collections::HashMap::new();
        if let Some(map) = result {
            for (k, v) in map {
                out.insert(
                    String::from_utf8_lossy(&k).to_string(),
                    String::from_utf8_lossy(&v).to_string(),
                );
            }
        }
        Ok(out)
    }

    fn vault_getShard(&self, key: String, node_id: String) -> Result<String> {
        let api = self.client.runtime_api();
        let result = api.vault_get_shard(key.into_bytes(), node_id.into_bytes());
        if let Some(bytes) = result {
            Ok(String::from_utf8_lossy(&bytes).to_string())
        } else {
            Ok(String::new())
        }
    }

    fn vault_getShardedDescriptor(
        &self,
        key: String
    ) -> Result<(u8, u8, Vec<String>)> {
        let api = self.client.runtime_api();
        let result = api.vault_get_sharded_descriptor(key.into_bytes());

        if let Some((total, threshold, node_ids)) = result {
            let ids = node_ids
                .into_iter()
                .map(|b| String::from_utf8_lossy(&b).to_string())
                .collect::<Vec<_>>();
            Ok((total, threshold, ids))
        } else {
            Ok((0, 0, Vec::new()))
        }
    }
}

from collections import defaultdict

def build_capital_heatmap(snapshot: CapitalSnapshot):
    by_regime = defaultdict(float)
    by_strategy = defaultdict(float)
    by_liquidation_risk = defaultdict(float)
    by_wave_state = defaultdict(float)
    by_snipe_state = defaultdict(float)

    for exp in snapshot.exposures:
        by_regime[exp.regime] += exp.notional_value
        by_strategy[exp.strategy] += exp.notional_value

        # Bucket liquidation risk
        if exp.liquidation_risk_score >= 0.7:
            by_liquidation_risk["high"] += exp.notional_value
        elif exp.liquidation_risk_score >= 0.4:
            by_liquidation_risk["medium"] += exp.notional_value
        else:
            by_liquidation_risk["low"] += exp.notional_value

        # Bucket wave continuation
        if exp.wave_continuation_score >= 0.7:
            by_wave_state["strong_wave"] += exp.notional_value
        elif exp.wave_continuation_score >= 0.4:
            by_wave_state["moderate_wave"] += exp.notional_value
        else:
            by_wave_state["weak_or_none"] += exp.notional_value

        # Bucket snipe opportunity (for symbols you *could* enter more)
        if exp.snipe_opportunity_score >= 0.7:
            by_snipe_state["high_opportunity"] += exp.notional_value
        elif exp.snipe_opportunity_score >= 0.4:
            by_snipe_state["medium_opportunity"] += exp.notional_value
        else:
            by_snipe_state["low_opportunity"] += exp.notional_value

    return {
        "by_regime": dict(by_regime),
        "by_strategy": dict(by_strategy),
        "by_liquidation_risk": dict(by_liquidation_risk),
        "by_wave_state": dict(by_wave_state),
        "by_snipe_state": dict(by_snipe_state),
        "cash_balance": snapshot.cash_balance,
        "total_equity": snapshot.total_equity,
    }
pub host_shortcode: BoundedVec<u8, T::MaxShortcodeLength>,

# d:/pqr.info/vault-adapter/adapter.py

import json
from http.server import BaseHTTPRequestHandler, HTTPServer
import requests

from discovery import discover_node
from shamir import recover_secret  # you’ll implement split/recover here

# Single "bootstrap" node for descriptor calls
BOOTSTRAP_RPC_URL = discover_node()


def call_rpc(url, method, params):
    payload = {
        "jsonrpc": "2.0",
        "id": 1,
        "method": method,
        "params": params,
    }
    r = requests.post(url, json=payload, timeout=3)
    r.raise_for_status()
    j = r.json()
    if "result" not in j:
        raise RuntimeError(f"RPC error: {j}")
    return j["result"]


class VaultAdapter(BaseHTTPRequestHandler):
    def _send(self, code, payload):
        self.send_response(code)
        self.send_header("Content-Type", "application/json")
        self.end_headers()
        self.wfile.write(json.dumps(payload).encode())

    def do_GET(self):
        if not self.path.startswith("/v1/secret/data/"):
            return self._send(404, {"error": "Unsupported path"})

        secret_key = self.path.replace("/v1/secret/data/", "")
        _vault_token = self.headers.get("X-Vault-Token", "")

        try:
            # 1) Get sharded descriptor from any healthy node
            desc = call_rpc(
                BOOTSTRAP_RPC_URL,
                "vault_getShardedDescriptor",
                [secret_key],
            )
            total, threshold, node_ids = desc  # (u8, u8, Vec<String>)

            # 2) For each node_id, map to RPC URL via discovery
            shards = []
            for node_id in node_ids:
                if len(shards) >= threshold:
                    break

                # For now, reuse discover_node() as a simple mapping.
                # In Phase 3.5 you can map node_id -> specific URL.
                node_url = discover_node()

                try:
                    shard = call_rpc(
                        node_url,
                        "vault_getShard",
                        [secret_key, node_id],
                    )
                    if shard:
                        shards.append(shard)
                except Exception:
                    continue

            if len(shards) < threshold:
                return self._send(500, {"error": "Not enough shards to reconstruct secret"})

            # 3) Reconstruct secret via Shamir
            secret_value = recover_secret(shards)

            # 4) Wrap into Vault KV v2 format
            vault_response = {
                "data": {
                    "data": {
                        "value": secret_value
                    }
                }
            }
            return self._send(200, vault_response)

        except Exception as e:
            return self._send(500, {"error": f"Adapter error: {str(e)}"})


def run():
    server = HTTPServer(("0.0.0.0", 8200), VaultAdapter)
    print("Vault Adapter (S27-Shamir) running on port 8200...")
    server.serve_forever()


if __name__ == "__main__":
    run()

syntax = "proto3";

package pqrld.v1;

// -------------------------------
// ENUMS
// -------------------------------

// Deterministic PQRL state machine states
enum RunlevelState {
  STATE_UNKNOWN       = 0;
  STATE_PENDING       = 1;
  STATE_PRECHECK      = 2;
  STATE_ACTIVATING    = 3;
  STATE_HEALTHCHECK   = 4;
  STATE_CONSTRAINED   = 5;
  STATE_RETRYING      = 6;
  STATE_ROLLBACK      = 7;
  STATE_FAILED        = 8;
  STATE_READY         = 9;
}

// -------------------------------
// MESSAGES
// -------------------------------

// Generic error surface
message Error {
  string message = 1;
  string details = 2;
}

// Request for runlevel status
message StatusRequest {
  int32 runlevel_id = 1; // 0–9
}

// Response containing runlevel status
message StatusResponse {
  int32 runlevel_id = 1;
  RunlevelState state = 2;
  string name = 3;
  string description = 4;
  string last_error = 5;
}

// Request to advance runlevel
message AdvanceRequest {
  int32 runlevel_id = 1;
}

// Response after attempting advancement
message AdvanceResponse {
  int32 runlevel_id = 1;
  RunlevelState state = 2;
  bool advanced = 3;
  Error error = 4;
}

// Request to rollback a runlevel
message RollbackRequest {
  int32 runlevel_id = 1;
}

// Response after rollback
message RollbackResponse {
  int32 runlevel_id = 1;
  RunlevelState state = 2;
  bool rolled_back = 3;
  Error error = 4;
}

// Trigger healing pipeline
message HealingRequest {
  int32 runlevel_id = 1;
  string reason = 2;
}

// Healing response
message HealingResponse {
  bool triggered = 1;
  string message = 2;
  Error error = 3;
}

// Reload TOML configuration
message ConfigRequest {}

// Response after config reload
message ConfigResponse {
  bool reloaded = 1;
  string message = 2;
  Error error = 3;
}

// -------------------------------
// SERVICE DEFINITION
// -------------------------------

service RunlevelService {

  // Get status of a specific runlevel
  rpc GetRunlevelStatus(StatusRequest) returns (StatusResponse);

  // Advance runlevel (only allowed if current is READY)
  rpc AdvanceRunlevel(AdvanceRequest) returns (AdvanceResponse);

  // Rollback runlevel (undo activation)
  rpc RollbackRunlevel(RollbackRequest) returns (RollbackResponse);

  // Trigger healing pipeline (HealerHookManager)
  rpc TriggerHealing(HealingRequest) returns (HealingResponse);

  // Reload TOML config from /etc/pqrl.d/*.toml
  rpc ReloadConfig(ConfigRequest) returns (ConfigResponse);
}

fn hash_addr5d(addr5d: &str) -> [u8; 16] {
    use blake2::Blake2s;
    use blake2::digest::{Update, VariableOutput};

    let mut hasher = Blake2s::new(16).unwrap();
    hasher.update(addr5d.as_bytes());
    let mut out = [0u8; 16];
    hasher.finalize_variable(|res| out.copy_from_slice(res));
    out
}

positions_with_market.json
// Below is the production‑grade config.go skeleton for
// SUBSTRATE/cmd/pqrld/config.go — exactly aligned with the PQRL.d spec you finalized.
// 
// It is:
// 
// deterministic
// 
// blocking‑ready
// 
// TOML‑driven
// 
// safe for containerized execution
// 
// structured for your existing executor.go FSM
// 
// fully compatible with /etc/pqrl.d/*.toml and /etc/sos/runlevels.toml
// 
// And it gives you the exact structs, loader, validator, and ordering logic your PQRL.d daemon needs.
// 
// 🟣 config.go — PQRL.d Runlevel Configuration Loader

package pqrld

import (
    "fmt"
    "os"
    "path/filepath"
    "sort"

    "github.com/pelletier/go-toml"
)

type Runlevel struct {
    ID          int               `toml:"id"`
    Name        string            `toml:"name"`
    Description string            `toml:"description"`

    Preconditions map[string]string `toml:"preconditions"`
    Activate      ActivateBlock     `toml:"activate"`
    Health        HealthBlock       `toml:"health"`
    Constraints   ConstraintBlock   `toml:"constraints"`
    Advance       AdvanceBlock      `toml:"advance"`
}

type ActivateBlock struct {
    Command    string   `toml:"command"`
    Container  string   `toml:"container"`
    DependsOn  []string `toml:"depends_on"`
}

type HealthBlock struct {
    GRPCPort         int    `toml:"grpc_port"`
    ContainerRunning bool   `toml:"container_running"`
    CheckEndpoint    string `toml:"check_endpoint"`
    TimeoutMS        int    `toml:"timeout_ms"`
}

type ConstraintBlock struct {
    RequireAll        bool   `toml:"require_all"`
    Retry             int    `toml:"retry"`
    RetryBackoffMS    int    `toml:"retry_backoff_ms"`
    RollbackOnFailure bool   `toml:"rollback_on_failure"`
    RollbackCommand   string `toml:"rollback_command"`
    EscalateTo        string `toml:"escalate_to"`
}

type AdvanceBlock struct {
    Next int `toml:"next"`
}

type Config struct {
    Runlevels []Runlevel
}

// LoadConfig loads all TOML files from /etc/pqrl.d and returns an ordered config.
func LoadConfig(dir string) (*Config, error) {
    var runlevels []Runlevel

    err := filepath.Walk(dir, func(path string, info os.FileInfo, err error) error {
        if err != nil {
            return err
        }
        if info.IsDir() || filepath.Ext(path) != ".toml" {
            return nil
        }

        raw, err := os.ReadFile(path)
        if err != nil {
            return fmt.Errorf("read %s: %w", path, err)
        }

        var rl Runlevel
        if err := toml.Unmarshal(raw, &rl); err != nil {
            return fmt.Errorf("parse %s: %w", path, err)
        }

        runlevels = append(runlevels, rl)
        return nil
    })

    if err != nil {
        return nil, err
    }

    if err := validateRunlevels(runlevels); err != nil {
        return nil, err
    }

    sort.Slice(runlevels, func(i, j int) bool {
        return runlevels[i].ID < runlevels[j].ID
    })

    return &Config{Runlevels: runlevels}, nil
}

// validateRunlevels ensures IDs are unique and sequential.
func validateRunlevels(rls []Runlevel) error {
    seen := make(map[int]bool)

    for _, rl := range rls {
        if rl.ID < 0 || rl.ID > 9 {
            return fmt.Errorf("invalid runlevel ID %d (must be 0–9)", rl.ID)
        }
        if seen[rl.ID] {
            return fmt.Errorf("duplicate runlevel ID %d", rl.ID)
        }
        seen[rl.ID] = true

        if rl.Advance.Next < rl.ID {
            return fmt.Errorf("runlevel %d cannot advance backwards to %d", rl.ID, rl.Advance.Next)
        }
    }

    return nil
}

// Below is the production‑grade config.go skeleton for
// SUBSTRATE/cmd/pqrld/config.go — exactly aligned with the PQRL.d spec you finalized.
// 
// It is:
// 
// deterministic
// 
// blocking‑ready
// 
// TOML‑driven
// 
// safe for containerized execution
// 
// structured for your existing executor.go FSM
// 
// fully compatible with /etc/pqrl.d/*.toml and /etc/sos/runlevels.toml
// 
// And it gives you the exact structs, loader, validator, and ordering logic your PQRL.d daemon needs.
// 
// 🟣 config.go — PQRL.d Runlevel Configuration Loader

package pqrld

import (
    "fmt"
    "os"
    "path/filepath"
    "sort"

    "github.com/pelletier/go-toml"
)

type Runlevel struct {
    ID          int               `toml:"id"`
    Name        string            `toml:"name"`
    Description string            `toml:"description"`

    Preconditions map[string]string `toml:"preconditions"`
    Activate      ActivateBlock     `toml:"activate"`
    Health        HealthBlock       `toml:"health"`
    Constraints   ConstraintBlock   `toml:"constraints"`
    Advance       AdvanceBlock      `toml:"advance"`
}

type ActivateBlock struct {
    Command    string   `toml:"command"`
    Container  string   `toml:"container"`
    DependsOn  []string `toml:"depends_on"`
}

type HealthBlock struct {
    GRPCPort         int    `toml:"grpc_port"`
    ContainerRunning bool   `toml:"container_running"`
    CheckEndpoint    string `toml:"check_endpoint"`
    TimeoutMS        int    `toml:"timeout_ms"`
}

type ConstraintBlock struct {
    RequireAll        bool   `toml:"require_all"`
    Retry             int    `toml:"retry"`
    RetryBackoffMS    int    `toml:"retry_backoff_ms"`
    RollbackOnFailure bool   `toml:"rollback_on_failure"`
    RollbackCommand   string `toml:"rollback_command"`
    EscalateTo        string `toml:"escalate_to"`
}

type AdvanceBlock struct {
    Next int `toml:"next"`
}

type Config struct {
    Runlevels []Runlevel
}

// LoadConfig loads all TOML files from /etc/pqrl.d and returns an ordered config.
func LoadConfig(dir string) (*Config, error) {
    var runlevels []Runlevel

    err := filepath.Walk(dir, func(path string, info os.FileInfo, err error) error {
        if err != nil {
            return err
        }
        if info.IsDir() || filepath.Ext(path) != ".toml" {
            return nil
        }

        raw, err := os.ReadFile(path)
        if err != nil {
            return fmt.Errorf("read %s: %w", path, err)
        }

        var rl Runlevel
        if err := toml.Unmarshal(raw, &rl); err != nil {
            return fmt.Errorf("parse %s: %w", path, err)
        }

        runlevels = append(runlevels, rl)
        return nil
    })

    if err != nil {
        return nil, err
    }

    if err := validateRunlevels(runlevels); err != nil {
        return nil, err
    }

    sort.Slice(runlevels, func(i, j int) bool {
        return runlevels[i].ID < runlevels[j].ID
    })

    return &Config{Runlevels: runlevels}, nil
}

// validateRunlevels ensures IDs are unique and sequential.
func validateRunlevels(rls []Runlevel) error {
    seen := make(map[int]bool)

    for _, rl := range rls {
        if rl.ID < 0 || rl.ID > 9 {
            return fmt.Errorf("invalid runlevel ID %d (must be 0–9)", rl.ID)
        }
        if seen[rl.ID] {
            return fmt.Errorf("duplicate runlevel ID %d", rl.ID)
        }
        seen[rl.ID] = true

        if rl.Advance.Next < rl.ID {
            return fmt.Errorf("runlevel %d cannot advance backwards to %d", rl.ID, rl.Advance.Next)
        }
    }

    return nil
}

    Command    string   `toml:"command"`

    RequireAll        bool   `toml:"require_all"`
    Retry             int    `toml:"retry"`

func LoadConfig(dir string) (*Config, error) {

            return err

            return nil
        }

        if err != nil {
            return fmt.Errorf("read %s: %w", path, err)
        }

        if err := toml.Unmarshal(raw, &rl); err != nil {
            return fmt.Errorf("parse %s: %w", path, err)
        }

        runlevels = append(runlevels, rl)

    if err != nil {
        return nil, err
    }

    if err := validateRunlevels(runlevels); err != nil {
        return nil, err
    }

    sort.Slice(runlevels, func(i, j int) bool {
        return runlevels[i].ID < runlevels[j].ID
    })

    return &Config{Runlevels: runlevels}, nil
}

// validateRunlevels ensures IDs are unique and sequential.
func validateRunlevels(rls []Runlevel) error {
    seen := make(map[int]bool)

    for _, rl := range rls {
        if rl.ID < 0 || rl.ID > 9 {
            return fmt.Errorf("invalid runlevel ID %d (must be 0–9)", rl.ID)
        }
        if seen[rl.ID] {
            return fmt.Errorf("duplicate runlevel ID %d", rl.ID)
        }
        seen[rl.ID] = true

        if rl.Advance.Next < rl.ID {
            return fmt.Errorf("runlevel %d cannot advance backwards to %d", rl.ID, rl.Advance.Next)
        }
    }

    return nil
}

const ALPHABET: &[u8] = b"ABCDEFGHIJKLMNOPQRSTUVWXYZ234567";
// See my thinking

fn encode_shortcode(addr5d: &str) -> String {
    let digest = hash_addr5d(addr5d);
    // treat digest as big integer, emit base32
    let mut value = 0u128;
    for b in digest.iter() {
        value = (value << 8) | (*b as u128);
    }

    let mut chars = Vec::new();
    for _ in 0..5 {
        let idx = (value & 0x1F) as usize; // 5 bits
        chars.push(ALPHABET[idx]);
        value >>= 5;
    }

    chars.reverse(); // most significant first
    String::from_utf8(chars).unwrap()
}

#[derive(Encode, Decode, Clone, PartialEq, Eq, TypeInfo)]
pub struct HostProfile<T: Config> {
    pub host_id: BoundedVec<u8, T::MaxHostIdLength>,
    pub metadata: BoundedVec<u8, T::MaxMetadataLength>,
    pub active: bool,
}

#[derive(Encode, Decode, Clone, PartialEq, Eq, TypeInfo)]
pub struct Lease<T: Config> {
    pub lease_id: u64,
    pub host_id: BoundedVec<u8, T::MaxHostIdLength>,
    pub process_fingerprint: BoundedVec<u8, T::MaxFingerprintLength>,
    pub role: BoundedVec<u8, T::MaxRoleLength>,
    pub addr5d: BoundedVec<u8, T::MaxAddr5dLength>,
    pub ports: Vec<u16>,
    pub ttl_epoch: u64,
}

#[derive(Encode, Decode, Clone, PartialEq, Eq, TypeInfo)]
pub struct RouteHint<T: Config> {
    pub from_addr5d: BoundedVec<u8, T::MaxAddr5dLength>,
    pub to_addr5d: BoundedVec<u8, T::MaxAddr5dLength>,
    pub hops: Vec<BoundedVec<u8, T::MaxAddr5dLength>>,
}

#[pallet::storage]
pub type Hosts<T> = StorageMap<
    _, Blake2_128Concat,
    BoundedVec<u8, T::MaxHostIdLength>,
    HostProfile<T>,
    OptionQuery
>;

#[pallet::storage]
pub type Leases<T> = StorageMap<
    _, Blake2_128Concat,
    u64,
    Lease<T>,
    OptionQuery
>;

#[pallet::storage]
pub type NextLeaseId<T> = StorageValue<_, u64, ValueQuery>;

#[pallet::storage]
pub type ReservedPorts<T> = StorageMap<
    _, Blake2_128Concat,
    BoundedVec<u8, T::MaxRoleLength>, // role
    u16,                              // global port
    OptionQuery
>;

#[pallet::storage]
pub type RouteHints<T> = StorageMap<
    _, Blake2_128Concat,
    BoundedVec<u8, T::MaxAddr5dLength>, // addr5d
    Vec<RouteHint<T>>,
    ValueQuery
>;

#[pallet::call]
impl<T: Config> Pallet<T> {
    // host registers itself
    pub fn register_host(
        origin: OriginFor<T>,
        host_id: BoundedVec<u8, T::MaxHostIdLength>,
        metadata: BoundedVec<u8, T::MaxMetadataLength>,
    ) -> DispatchResult { /* ... */ }

    // process asks for a lease: 5D addr + ports + ttl
    pub fn request_lease(
        origin: OriginFor<T>,
        host_id: BoundedVec<u8, T::MaxHostIdLength>,
        process_fingerprint: BoundedVec<u8, T::MaxFingerprintLength>,
        role: BoundedVec<u8, T::MaxRoleLength>,
    ) -> DispatchResult { /* ... */ }

    pub fn renew_lease(
        origin: OriginFor<T>,
        lease_id: u64,
    ) -> DispatchResult { /* ... */ }

    pub fn release_lease(
        origin: OriginFor<T>,
        lease_id: u64,
    ) -> DispatchResult { /* ... */ }

    // route hints updated by mesh controller / off-chain worker
    pub fn push_route_hint(
        origin: OriginFor<T>,
        hint: RouteHint<T>,
    ) -> DispatchResult { /* ... */ }
}

QJX9T → addr5d → IPv6
const ALPHABET: &[u8] = b"ABCDEFGHIJKLMNOPQRSTUVWXYZ234567";

fn hash_addr5d(addr5d: &str) -> [u8; 16] {
    use blake2::Blake2s;
    use blake2::digest::{Update, VariableOutput};

    let mut hasher = Blake2s::new(16).unwrap();
    hasher.update(addr5d.as_bytes());
    let mut out = [0u8; 16];
    hasher.finalize_variable(|res| out.copy_from_slice(res));
    out
}

fn encode_shortcode(addr5d: &str) -> String {
    let digest = hash_addr5d(addr5d);

    let mut value = 0u128;
    for b in digest.iter() {
        value = (value << 8) | (*b as u128);
    }

    let mut chars = Vec::new();
    for _ in 0..5 {
        let idx = (value & 0x1F) as usize;
        chars.push(ALPHABET[idx]);
        value >>= 5;
    }

    chars.reverse();
    String::from_utf8(chars).unwrap()
}

--text-primary
// ===============================
// ImageFX Thesaurus Module
// ===============================

const promptInput = document.getElementById("prompt");

// Create floating synonym dropdown
const synonymBox = document.createElement("div");
synonymBox.id = "synonym-box";
synonymBox.style.position = "absolute";
synonymBox.style.display = "none";
synonymBox.style.zIndex = "9999";
synonymBox.style.background = "var(--panel-bg)";
synonymBox.style.color = "var(--text-primary)";
synonymBox.style.border = "1px solid var(--border-color)";
synonymBox.style.borderRadius = "var(--radius)";
synonymBox.style.padding = "6px";
synonymBox.style.fontSize = "0.9em";
synonymBox.style.boxShadow = "0 2px 6px rgba(0,0,0,0.3)";
document.body.appendChild(synonymBox);


// Utility: get selected word inside input
function getSelectedWord() {
  const input = promptInput;
  const start = input.selectionStart;
  const end = input.selectionEnd;

  if (start === end) return null;

  const text = input.value;
  return text.substring(start, end);
}


// Utility: replace selected word with synonym
function replaceSelectedWord(newWord) {
  const input = promptInput;
  const start = input.selectionStart;
  const end = input.selectionEnd;

  const before = input.value.substring(0, start);
  const after = input.value.substring(end);

  input.value = before + newWord + after;

  // Move cursor to end of inserted word
  const cursorPos = before.length + newWord.length;
  input.setSelectionRange(cursorPos, cursorPos);
  input.focus();
}


// Position synonym dropdown near cursor
function positionSynonymBox(event) {
  synonymBox.style.left = `${event.clientX + 10}px`;
  synonymBox.style.top = `${event.clientY + 10}px`;
}


// Fetch synonyms from Datamuse
async function fetchSynonyms(word) {
  try {
    const res = await fetch(`https://api.datamuse.com/words?ml=${word}`);
    const data = await res.json();
    return data.slice(0, 8).map(item => item.word); // limit to 8 synonyms
  } catch (err) {
    console.error("Thesaurus error:", err);
    return [];
  }
}


// Render synonym dropdown
function showSynonymDropdown(synonyms, event) {
  synonymBox.innerHTML = "";

  if (synonyms.length === 0) {
    synonymBox.innerHTML = "<div style='opacity:0.6'>No synonyms found</div>";
  } else {
    synonyms.forEach(syn => {
      const item = document.createElement("div");
      item.textContent = syn;
      item.style.padding = "4px 6px";
      item.style.cursor = "pointer";

      item.addEventListener("click", () => {
        replaceSelectedWord(syn);
        synonymBox.style.display = "none";
      });

      item.addEventListener("mouseover", () => {
        item.style.background = "var(--accent)";
        item.style.color = "#fff";
      });

      item.addEventListener("mouseout", () => {
        item.style.background = "var(--panel-bg)";
        item.style.color = "var(--text-primary)";
      });

      synonymBox.appendChild(item);
    });
  }

  positionSynonymBox(event);
  synonymBox.style.display = "block";
}


// Hide dropdown when clicking outside
document.addEventListener("click", (e) => {
  if (!synonymBox.contains(e.target)) {
    synonymBox.style.display = "none";
  }
});


// Main interaction: double‑click word → synonyms
promptInput.addEventListener("dblclick", async (event) => {
  const word = getSelectedWord();
  if (!word || word.trim().length === 0) return;

  const synonyms = await fetchSynonyms(word);
  showSynonymDropdown(synonyms, event);
});
// ===============================
// ImageFX Thesaurus Module
// ===============================

  }
}


// Render synonym dropdown
function showSynonymDropdown(synonyms, event) {
  synonymBox.innerHTML = "";

  if (synonyms.length === 0) {
    synonymBox.innerHTML = "<div style='opacity:0.6'>No synonyms found</div>";
  } else {
    synonyms.forEach(syn => {
      const item = document.createElement("div");
      item.textContent = syn;
      item.style.padding = "4px 6px";
      item.style.cursor = "pointer";

      item.addEventListener("click", () => {
        replaceSelectedWord(syn);
        synonymBox.style.display = "none";
      });

      item.addEventListener("mouseover", () => {
        item.style.background = "var(--accent)";
        item.style.color = "#fff";
      });

      item.addEventListener("mouseout", () => {
        item.style.background = "var(--panel-bg)";
        item.style.color = "var(--text-primary)";
      });

      synonymBox.appendChild(item);
    });
  }

  positionSynonymBox(event);
  synonymBox.style.display = "block";
}


// Hide dropdown when clicking outside
document.addEventListener("click", (e) => {
  if (!synonymBox.contains(e.target)) {
    synonymBox.style.display = "none";
  }
});


// Main interaction: double‑click word → synonyms
promptInput.addEventListener("dblclick", async (event) => {
  const word = getSelectedWord();
  if (!word || word.trim().length === 0) return;

  const synonyms = await fetchSynonyms(word);
  showSynonymDropdown(synonyms, event);
});
// ===============================
// ImageFX Thesaurus Module
// ===============================

const promptInput = document.getElementById("prompt");

// Create floating synonym dropdown
const synonymBox = document.createElement("div");
synonymBox.id = "synonym-box";
synonymBox.style.position = "absolute";
synonymBox.style.display = "none";
synonymBox.style.zIndex = "9999";
synonymBox.style.background = "var(--panel-bg)";
synonymBox.style.color = "var(--text-primary)";
synonymBox.style.border = "1px solid var(--border-color)";
synonymBox.style.borderRadius = "var(--radius)";
synonymBox.style.padding = "6px";
synonymBox.style.fontSize = "0.9em";
synonymBox.style.boxShadow = "0 2px 6px rgba(0,0,0,0.3)";
document.body.appendChild(synonymBox);


// Utility: get selected word inside input
function getSelectedWord() {
  const input = promptInput;
  const start = input.selectionStart;
  const end = input.selectionEnd;

  if (start === end) return null;

  const text = input.value;
  return text.substring(start, end);
}


// Utility: replace selected word with synonym
function replaceSelectedWord(newWord) {
  const input = promptInput;
  const start = input.selectionStart;
  const end = input.selectionEnd;

  const before = input.value.substring(0, start);
  const after = input.value.substring(end);

  input.value = before + newWord + after;

  // Move cursor to end of inserted word
  const cursorPos = before.length + newWord.length;
  input.setSelectionRange(cursorPos, cursorPos);
  input.focus();
}


// Position synonym dropdown near cursor
function positionSynonymBox(event) {
  synonymBox.style.left = `${event.clientX + 10}px`;
  synonymBox.style.top = `${event.clientY + 10}px`;
}


// Fetch synonyms from Datamuse
async function fetchSynonyms(word) {
  try {
    const res = await fetch(`https://api.datamuse.com/words?ml=${word}`);
    const data = await res.json();
    return data.slice(0, 8).map(item => item.word); // limit to 8 synonyms
  } catch (err) {
    console.error("Thesaurus error:", err);
    return [];
  }
}


// Render synonym dropdown
function showSynonymDropdown(synonyms, event) {
  synonymBox.innerHTML = "";

  if (synonyms.length === 0) {
    synonymBox.innerHTML = "<div style='opacity:0.6'>No synonyms found</div>";
  } else {
    synonyms.forEach(syn => {
      const item = document.createElement("div");
      item.textContent = syn;
      item.style.padding = "4px 6px";
      item.style.cursor = "pointer";

      item.addEventListener("click", () => {
        replaceSelectedWord(syn);
        synonymBox.style.display = "none";
      });

      item.addEventListener("mouseover", () => {
        item.style.background = "var(--accent)";
        item.style.color = "#fff";
      });

      item.addEventListener("mouseout", () => {
        item.style.background = "var(--panel-bg)";
        item.style.color = "var(--text-primary)";
      });

      synonymBox.appendChild(item);
    });
  }

  positionSynonymBox(event);
  synonymBox.style.display = "block";
}


// Hide dropdown when clicking outside
document.addEventListener("click", (e) => {
  if (!synonymBox.contains(e.target)) {
    synonymBox.style.display = "none";
  }
});


// Main interaction: double‑click word → synonyms
promptInput.addEventListener("dblclick", async (event) => {
  const word = getSelectedWord();
  if (!word || word.trim().length === 0) return;

  const synonyms = await fetchSynonyms(word);
  showSynonymDropdown(synonyms, event);
});

function setTheme(name) {
  document.documentElement.classList.remove(
    "theme-modern-dark",
    "theme-modern-light",
    "theme-classic"
  );
  document.documentElement.classList.add(name);
  localStorage.setItem("imagefx-theme", name);
}

const saved = localStorage.getItem("imagefx-theme") || "theme-modern-dark";
setTheme(saved);

// Example: hook up to a toggle
document.getElementById("theme-toggle-modern").onclick = () =>
  setTheme("theme-modern-dark");
document.getElementById("theme-toggle-light").onclick = () =>
  setTheme("theme-modern-light");
document.getElementById("theme-toggle-classic").onclick = () =>
  setTheme("theme-classic");

prompt.addEventListener("dblclick", async (e) => {
  const word = getSelectedWord();
  const res = await fetch(`https://api.datamuse.com/words?ml=${word}`);
  const synonyms = await res.json();
  showSynonymDropdown(synonyms.map(s => s.word));
});

const ALPHABET: &[u8] = b"ABCDEFGHIJKLMNOPQRSTUVWXYZ234567";

fn hash_addr5d(addr5d: &[u8]) -> [u8; 16] {
    use blake2::Blake2s;
    use blake2::digest::{Update, VariableOutput};

    let mut hasher = Blake2s::new(16).expect("Blake2s init");
    hasher.update(addr5d);
    let mut out = [0u8; 16];
    hasher.finalize_variable(|res| out.copy_from_slice(res));
    out
}

fn encode_shortcode_from_bytes(addr5d: &[u8]) -> Vec<u8> {
    let digest = hash_addr5d(addr5d);

    let mut value = 0u128;
    for b in digest.iter() {
        value = (value << 8) | (*b as u128);
    }

    let mut chars = Vec::new();
    for _ in 0..5 {
        let idx = (value & 0x1F) as usize;
        chars.push(ALPHABET[idx]);
        value >>= 5;
    }

    chars.reverse();
    chars
}

pub struct LeaseShortcodeMigration<T>(sp_std::marker::PhantomData<T>);

impl<T: Config> frame_support::traits::OnRuntimeUpgrade for LeaseShortcodeMigration<T> {
    fn on_runtime_upgrade() -> frame_support::weights::Weight {
        use frame_support::storage::StorageMap;
        use frame_support::traits::Get;

        let mut weight: frame_support::weights::Weight = 0;

        // Assuming Leases<T> is your storage map: LeaseId -> Lease<T>
        Leases::<T>::translate(|_lease_id, mut lease: Lease<T>| {
            // Compute shortcode from addr5d
            let shortcode_vec = encode_shortcode_from_bytes(&lease.addr5d);

            // Bound it
            if let Ok(bounded) =
                BoundedVec::<u8, T::MaxShortcodeLength>::try_from(shortcode_vec)
            {
                lease.shortcode = bounded;
            }

            weight += T::DbWeight::get().reads_writes(1, 1);
            Some(lease)
        });

        weight
    }
}

pub struct VaultShortcodeMigration<T>(sp_std::marker::PhantomData<T>);

impl<T: Config> frame_support::traits::OnRuntimeUpgrade for VaultShortcodeMigration<T> {
    fn on_runtime_upgrade() -> frame_support::weights::Weight {
        use frame_support::storage::StorageMap;
        use frame_support::traits::Get;

        let mut weight: frame_support::weights::Weight = 0;

        VaultSecrets::<T>::translate(|_id, mut desc: VaultSecretDescriptor<T>| {
            let shortcode_vec = encode_shortcode_from_bytes(&desc.addr5d);

            if let Ok(bounded) =
                BoundedVec::<u8, T::MaxShortcodeLength>::try_from(shortcode_vec)
            {
                desc.shortcode = bounded;
            }

            weight += T::DbWeight::get().reads_writes(1, 1);
            Some(desc)
        });

        weight
    }
}
